# Video Agent 工作区说明

本目录用于集中维护 Hermes 项目内所有和“视频生成 Agent”相关的内容，包括制片人 Agent、子 Skill、Mock Skill、Schema、模板、审核规范、视频生成会话产物和后续产品化设计文档。

## 目录结构

## 子目录职责

### skills/

存放视频 Agent 相关的 Hermes Skills。

建议先维护以下 Mock Skills：

- video-producer：制片人主控 Skill，负责调度、状态管理、审核门和用户交互。
- mock-scriptwriter：兼容保留旧目录名的剧本 Skill，已融合 short-drama 微短剧编剧方法论；根据 approved brief 生成强钩子、强冲突、可分镜化且严格符合 `script.schema.json` 的 `script/script_v{N}.md/json`。
- mock-storyboard：Mock 分镜 Skill，用于根据剧本生成固定结构的分镜表。
- asset-planner：资产提取、资产参考图生成、整套分镜图片生成 Skill；上游读取 scriptwriter 输出的 `script/script_v{N}.*` 与 storyboard 输出的 `storyboard/storyboard_v{N}.*`，按 `assets-manifest.schema.json` 生成 `assets/assets_manifest_v{N}.md/json`，把可复用资产图片保存到 `assets/generated/v{N}/`，并把路径索引写入 `assets/generated/assets_generation_queue_v{N}.json` 与 `assets/generated/assets_generation_status_v{N}.json`；把整套分镜图片保存到 `assets/storyboard_frames/v{N}/`，并把逐镜头可解析索引写入 `assets/storyboard_frames/storyboard_frame_queue_v{N}.json`、`storyboard_frame_status_v{N}.json`、`storyboard_frames_manifest_v{N}.json/md`。`assets_manifest_v{N}.json` 严格遵守 schema，不写 image_path/local_path/url 等额外字段。
- doubao-seedance-shot-generator：豆包 Seedance 2.0 视频片段生成参数组织 Skill，用于根据 storyboard 和 assets 生成逐镜头 API 请求参数包；默认不直接提交 API。
- doubao-seedance-shot-submitter：豆包 Seedance 2.0 视频片段逐个提交 Skill；一次只提交一个 shot，等待该 shot 生成结果和人工效果确认后才允许继续下一个 shot。
- dreamina-cli：即梦安装器提供的官方 Dreamina CLI Skill，作为 `dreamina` 命令的通用说明和能力索引。
- dreamina-shot-submitter：项目内适配的即梦逐镜头提交 Skill；基于 `dreamina` Generator Commands 自动选择 `text2video` / `multimodal2video` / `image2video` / `multiframe2video` 等执行 one-shot-at-a-time 视频生成队列；默认模型为 `seedance2.0fast_vip`，但每次真实提交前必须向用户确认本次 `model_version`。
- simple-video-editor：项目内混剪/剪辑测试 Skill；使用 ffmpeg 将已生成且可用于测试的 shot 视频拼接成 rough cut，支持只用部分 shots 做 edit skill MVP 测试。
- reviewers：自动审核规则集合，可拆成 script-reviewer、storyboard-reviewer、asset-reviewer、shot-task-reviewer。

组员后续交付真实 Skill 时，建议先放入 skills/ 下单独目录，再由 video-producer 适配调用。

### schemas/

存放所有机器可读产物的 JSON Schema。所有子 Skill 输出 JSON 前，应以这些 schema 为契约。

新增视频片段生成阶段后，`shot-generation-tasks.schema.json` 约束 `shots/shot_tasks_v{N}.json`，用于保存豆包 Seedance 2.0 的逐镜头请求体、curl 模板和覆盖检查结果。

新增混剪/剪辑测试阶段后，`edit-manifest.schema.json` 约束 `edit/edit_manifest_v{N}.json`，用于记录 rough cut 输入片段、输出视频、partial edit test 覆盖范围和 ffprobe 结果。

### templates/

存放会话初始化、状态文件、人审记录、最终制作包等模板。

### docs/

存放流程说明、字段契约、人审规范和产品化路线图。

### video-generation-session/

存放每一个视频主题及其多集/多段剧本 session 的所有产物。

从现在开始，推荐使用两层目录：

```text
video-agent/video-generation-session/{topic_name}/{topic_name}_no{episode_number}_{yyyymmdd}/
```

含义：

- `topic_name`：主题/系列名，例如 `cyber_cat_trailer`。
- `no{episode_number}`：该主题下第几段剧本或第几集，例如 `no1`、`no2`。
- `yyyymmdd`：该段剧本 session 创建日期。

例如：

```text
video-agent/video-generation-session/cyber_cat_trailer/cyber_cat_trailer_no1_20260503/
video-agent/video-generation-session/cyber_cat_trailer/cyber_cat_trailer_no2_20260506/
```

这样同一个主题下的后续剧情会自然聚合在同一个主题目录里，比 `cyber_cat_trailer_continuation_20260506` 更容易区分系列和集数。

旧版扁平目录，例如：

```text
video-agent/video-generation-session/{legacy_session_name}_{yyyymmdd}/
```

如果历史项目里仍有这种目录，它们仍然可以按 legacy session 恢复；新建主题和续写 session 默认使用两层目录。

后续该视频生成 session 产生的所有文件都必须保存在对应 `{topic_name}/{topic_name}_no{N}_{yyyymmdd}/` 目录下。

在进入 `brief` 阶段前，WebUI 输入口收到的用户剧本描述、补充说明、图片、语音、视频、文档等原始素材必须先保存到当前 session 的 `user_assets/` 目录，并生成 `user_assets/user_assets_v001.md` 与 `user_assets/user_assets_v001.json`。后续 `brief`、`script`、`storyboard`、`assets` 等阶段只能通过这些 session-relative 路径引用用户原始素材，不应只依赖聊天上下文或临时上传 URL。

如果会话中断，可通过 `topic_name + no + date` 恢复该视频生成会话。

## 命名规范

- 主题名称建议使用英文小写、数字和下划线，例如 `cyber_cat_trailer`。
- 同一主题下的剧本/集数使用 `no1`、`no2`、`no3` 递增。
- 日期使用 yyyymmdd，例如 20260503。
- 推荐主题目录格式：`video-generation-session/{topic_name}/`。
- 推荐 session 目录名格式：`{topic_name}_no{episode_number}_{yyyymmdd}`。
- 完整 session 路径格式：`video-generation-session/{topic_name}/{topic_name}_no{episode_number}_{yyyymmdd}/`。
- 旧版扁平目录 `{session_name}_{yyyymmdd}` 仅作为 legacy session 继续支持，不作为新建默认规则。
- 阶段产物版本号使用 `v001`、`v002`、`v003`。
- 每个阶段至少输出 Markdown 和 JSON 两类产物：Markdown 给人审，JSON 给流程读取。

## 阶段产物原则

每个阶段都应遵循：

```text
生成产物 -> 自动审核 -> 人工审核 -> 批准后进入下一阶段
```

禁止在未获得人工确认前进入下一阶段。

如果上游产物发生变化，下游产物必须标记为 stale，并重新生成或重新审核。

## 从新建 session 到视频生成到打包的完整流程

接入真实视频片段生成和混剪后，推荐的完整阶段链路是：

```text
user_assets -> brief -> script -> storyboard -> assets -> shots -> edit -> package -> package_complete
```

其中每个阶段的位置和职责如下：

| 阶段 | 主要职责 | 主要 Skill / 工具 | 主要产物 |
|---|---|---|---|
| user_assets | 保存 WebUI 输入口收到的原始剧本描述、文本需求和上传文件，并为后续阶段提供可解析素材索引 | video-producer | `user_assets/user_assets_v{N}.md/json`, `user_assets/uploads/`, `user_assets/transcripts/`, `user_assets/metadata/`, `user_assets/derived/` |
| brief | 把用户需求规范化成可执行 brief | video-producer | `brief/user_brief.md`, `brief/normalized_brief.json`, `brief/review_v001.*` |
| script | 生成剧本、角色、场景、beats、旁白计划 | mock-scriptwriter 或其他真实 scriptwriter | `script/script_v{N}.md/json`, `script/review_v{N}.*` |
| storyboard | 把剧本拆成镜头表 | mock-storyboard 或真实 storyboard skill | `storyboard/storyboard_v{N}.md/json/csv`, `storyboard/review_v{N}.*` |
| assets | 根据 scriptwriter 剧本和 storyboard 分镜提取人物、场景、道具、画面/FX 等资产，生成资产参考图和整套分镜图片 | asset-planner | `assets/assets_manifest_v{N}.md/json`, `assets/generated/v{N}/`, `assets/generated/assets_generation_queue_v{N}.json`, `assets/generated/assets_generation_status_v{N}.json`, `assets/storyboard_frames/v{N}/`, `assets/storyboard_frames/storyboard_frame_queue_v{N}.json`, `assets/storyboard_frames/storyboard_frame_status_v{N}.json`, `assets/storyboard_frames/storyboard_frames_manifest_v{N}.json/md`, `assets/review_v{N}.*` |
| shots request package | 根据 storyboard + assets 组织视频生成请求参数，不直接消耗 credits | doubao-seedance-shot-generator 或其他 provider request generator | `shots/shot_tasks_v{N}.md/json`, `shots/review_v{N}.*` |
| shots submission / generation | 真实提交视频片段生成任务，一次只提交一个 shot，等待生成和人工效果确认后再继续 | dreamina-shot-submitter 或 doubao-seedance-shot-submitter | `shots/dreamina_submission_v{N}.*`, `shots/dreamina_responses/*`, `shots/downloads/{shot_id}/*.mp4` |
| edit / mixdown | 把已生成视频片段混剪、拼接、转场、后续可加 BGM/旁白/字幕 | simple-video-editor / ffmpeg | `edit/edit_manifest_v{N}.*`, `edit/rough_cut_v{N}.mp4`, `edit/review_v{N}.*` |
| package | 汇总本次 session 的最终产物、版本、审核历史、限制和下一步建议 | video-producer | `reports/production_package.md`, `reports/production_package_v{N}.md` |

### shots 阶段为什么拆成 request package 和 submission

视频片段生成分为两层：

1. `shot generator` / request package：只根据 storyboard 和 assets 生成每个 shot 的请求体、prompt、模型参数、curl/CLI 模板和覆盖检查，不调用外部服务，不消耗 credits。
2. `shot submitter` / submission：在用户批准 shot request package 后，才真实调用 Dreamina CLI 或其他 provider，且必须 one-shot-at-a-time。

这样做的原因：

- 可以先人审每个 shot 的 prompt 和参数，避免错误请求直接消耗 credits。
- 可以确保每个 storyboard shot 都有对应任务，且没有遗漏资产引用。
- 可以逐个生成、逐个确认效果，避免一次性生成 9 个错误片段。

# Video Agent Workflow

Canonical stage chain:

```text
brief -> script -> storyboard -> assets -> shots -> edit -> package -> package_complete
```

Every stage follows:

```text
generate artifacts -> automatic review -> human review -> approved before next stage
```

## Session Root

New sessions use:

```text
video-generation-session/{topic_name}/{topic_name}_no{episode_number}_{yyyymmdd}/
```

All artifacts for a session must remain under that session root.

## Assets to Shots Handoff

The assets stage hands off three classes of files to later skills:

1. Schema manifest: `assets/assets_manifest_v{N}.json`
2. Reusable asset image index: `assets/generated/assets_generation_status_v{N}.json`
3. Full storyboard frame index: `assets/storyboard_frames/storyboard_frames_manifest_v{N}.json`

Shot request generators must read the manifest for asset semantics and shot-to-asset mapping, then read sidecar indexes for actual local still paths. They must not scrape Markdown as the only source of truth, and must not add image paths back into the schema manifest.

## Review Gate

`assets/review_v{N}.json` can pass only when:

- `assets_manifest_v{N}.json` parses and validates against `schemas/assets-manifest.schema.json`.
- Every storyboard shot appears in `shot_asset_map`.
- Every generated/approved asset with a local image has a matching sidecar entry and a file that exists and opens.
- Every generated storyboard frame listed in the frame manifest has a file that exists and opens.
- No stale upstream artifact is being used.

## Downstream Use

If local asset/frame stills are not uploaded to a provider-accessible URL, video request packages must stay text-only or record local paths in notes for a later upload step. Provider API `image_url` fields must never contain a local filesystem path.

# Video Agent Artifact Contracts

## User Asset Stage Contract

The `user_assets` stage is produced by `skills/video-producer/SKILL.md` before brief normalization. It captures the raw WebUI/API/CLI text request plus uploaded images, audio, video, and documents under the active session root:

```text
{session_root}/user_assets/
```

Canonical files:

```text
user_assets/user_assets_v{N}.json
user_assets/user_assets_v{N}.md
```

`user_assets/user_assets_v{N}.json` must validate against `schemas/user-assets.schema.json`. Uploaded files must be copied or explicitly marked pending under session-relative paths:

```text
user_assets/uploads/{asset_id}_{safe_original_name}
user_assets/transcripts/{asset_id}_transcript.md
user_assets/metadata/{asset_id}.json
user_assets/derived/{asset_id}_summary.md
```

If the runtime cannot access an uploaded file blob, do not pretend it was copied. Add an `uploaded_assets[]` entry with a pending `stored_path` such as `user_assets/uploads/pending_user_assets_001_reference.png`, describe the issue in Markdown, and keep downstream stages aware that the file is missing until resolved.

Downstream skills should use `user_assets/user_assets_v{N}.json` as the machine-readable index for user-provided references. Markdown is for human review only and must not be the sole source of a user-uploaded asset path.

## Assets Stage Contract

The assets stage is produced by `skills/asset-planner/SKILL.md` and lives entirely under the active session root:

```text
{session_root}/assets/
```

Canonical schema-governed files:

```text
assets/assets_manifest_v{N}.json
assets/assets_manifest_v{N}.md
```

`assets/assets_manifest_v{N}.json` must validate against `schemas/assets-manifest.schema.json`. Because that schema uses `additionalProperties: false`, it must not contain image path or URL helper fields such as `image_path`, `local_path`, `url`, `file`, `generated_image`, or `reference_image`.

## Reusable Asset Images

Reusable asset images are saved under:

```text
assets/generated/v{N}/{asset_id}_{asset_key}.png
```

Required sidecar indexes in production image mode:

```text
assets/generated/assets_generation_queue_v{N}.json
assets/generated/assets_generation_status_v{N}.json
```

Downstream skills resolve reusable asset image paths from `assets_generation_status_v{N}.json.generated[]` by matching `asset_id` or `asset_key`. All stored paths are session-relative, for example:

```json
{
  "asset_id": "asset_001",
  "asset_key": "char_001_turnaround",
  "path": "assets/generated/v001/asset_001_char_001_turnaround.png",
  "size": [1024, 1536],
  "bytes": 123456,
  "qc_status": "pending_manual_qc"
}
```

## Storyboard Frame Images

Cinematic storyboard stills are saved under:

```text
assets/storyboard_frames/v{N}/{shot_id}_{variant}.png
```

Variants use `A`, `B`, `C`, `D`:

- `A`: hero keyframe / most representative shot beat.
- `B`: setup, before-state, insert, or asset-context frame.
- `C`: result, after-state, reaction, or continuity-completion frame.
- `D`: optional extra detail frame.

Required sidecar indexes when storyboard frames are planned or generated:

```text
assets/storyboard_frames/storyboard_frame_queue_v{N}.json
assets/storyboard_frames/storyboard_frame_status_v{N}.json
assets/storyboard_frames/storyboard_frames_manifest_v{N}.json
assets/storyboard_frames/storyboard_frames_manifest_v{N}.md
```

Downstream skills resolve complete shot still references from `storyboard_frames_manifest_v{N}.json.shots[]` by matching `shot_id`.

## Markdown Alignment Rule

Markdown files are for human review, but every image path shown in Markdown must also appear in a sidecar JSON index. Do not make Markdown the only source of an image path.

## Path Resolution Rule

All artifact paths written inside session files should be session-relative. A consumer resolves them by joining:

```text
{session_root}/{session_relative_path}
```

Do not store temporary cache URLs as final artifacts. Do not store paths outside the active session root.

# Video Agent Implementation Plan

> For Hermes: 后续实现本计划时，优先使用 subagent-driven-development / test-driven-development 思路逐步落地；但当前阶段先以 Hermes Skills + 文件化工作流跑通 MVP。

Goal: 在 Hermes 项目内实现一个“制片人 Agent + 子 Skill + 文件化中间产物 + 自动审核 + 人工审核”的视频生成 Agent 框架。

Architecture: 所有视频 Agent 相关内容集中放在 `video-agent/` 目录。制片人 Agent 是主控，负责创建视频生成会话、调度 mock/真实子 Skill、维护 `production_state.json`、执行自动审核、请求人工审核，并根据反馈返工或推进下一阶段。每次视频生成会话独立保存在 `video-agent/video-generation-session/{session_name}_{yyyymmdd}/` 下。

Tech Stack: Hermes Skills、Hermes file/terminal/delegation/clarify 工具、Markdown、JSON、JSON Schema；后续产品化阶段接入视频生成 API、图像生成 API、ffmpeg、对象存储、任务队列或工作流引擎。

---

## 0. 当前共识

我们当前选择的实现路线是：

```text
方案 B：Hermes Skills + 文件化工作流
```

第一阶段不追求真实视频生成能力，而是先 mock：

1. scriptwriter skill
2. storyboard skill
3. asset-planner skill

核心目标是跑通链路：

```text
用户输入需求
-> 创建视频生成会话目录
-> 规范化 brief
-> 人工确认 brief
-> mock 剧本生成
-> 自动审核剧本
-> 人工审核剧本
-> mock 分镜生成
-> 自动审核分镜
-> 人工审核分镜
-> mock 资产规划
-> 自动审核资产规划
-> 人工审核资产规划
-> 输出制作包 production_package.md
```

---

## 1. 顶层目录规划

所有视频 Agent 相关内容统一放在：

```text
video-agent/
```

建议结构：

```text
video-agent/
  README.md
  implementation-plan.md

  skills/
    video-producer/
      SKILL.md
    mock-scriptwriter/
      SKILL.md
    mock-storyboard/
      SKILL.md
    asset-planner/
      SKILL.md
    reviewers/
      script-reviewer.md
      storyboard-reviewer.md
      asset-reviewer.md

  schemas/
    brief.schema.json
    script.schema.json
    storyboard.schema.json
    assets-manifest.schema.json
    review.schema.json
    production-state.schema.json

  templates/
    user-brief-template.md
    production-state-template.json
    human-review-log-template.md
    production-package-template.md

  docs/
    workflow.md
    artifact-contracts.md
    human-review-guidelines.md
    productization-roadmap.md

  video-generation-session/
    {session_name}_{yyyymmdd}/
      production_state.json
      brief/
      script/
      storyboard/
      assets/
      reviews/
      reports/
```

### 关于子 Skill 是否放在 video-agent 目录下

建议 MVP 阶段先放在：

```text
video-agent/skills/
```

原因：

1. 方便视频 Agent 项目内集中管理。
2. 方便 mock skill 和真实 skill 并存。
3. 方便组员交付后统一适配。
4. 避免一开始污染全局 `~/.hermes/skills/`。

后续如果某个 skill 稳定成熟，再考虑安装/同步到 Hermes 全局 Skills 目录。

---

## 2. 视频生成会话目录规范

用户开启一个全新视频生成会话时，必须先输入：

```text
本次计划名称
```

系统根据：

```text
{计划名称}_{yyyymmdd}
```

创建会话目录：

```text
video-agent/video-generation-session/{session_name}_{yyyymmdd}/
```

例子：

```text
video-agent/video-generation-session/cyber_cat_trailer_20260429/
```

### 计划名称规范

计划名称建议 normalize：

- 中文可转拼音或保留但建议另存 display_name。
- 文件夹名建议只使用小写英文、数字、下划线、短横线。
- 空格替换为下划线。
- 去掉特殊字符。

推荐字段：

```json
{
  "session_id": "cyber_cat_trailer_20260429",
  "display_name": "赛博朋克猫侦探预告片",
  "session_name": "cyber_cat_trailer",
  "date": "20260429"
}
```

### 恢复会话

如果会话中断，用户可以说：

```text
继续 cyber_cat_trailer_20260429
```

制片人 Agent 应读取：

```text
video-agent/video-generation-session/cyber_cat_trailer_20260429/production_state.json
```

并根据状态继续下一步。

---

## 3. 单次会话内部目录结构

每次视频生成会话创建后，初始化：

```text
{session_root}/
  production_state.json

  brief/
    user_brief.md
    normalized_brief.json
    review_v001.md
    review_v001.json

  script/
    script_v001.md
    script_v001.json
    review_v001.md
    review_v001.json

    storyboard_v001.json
    storyboard_v001.csv
    review_v001.md
    review_v001.json

  assets/
    assets_manifest_v001.md
    assets_manifest_v001.json
    review_v001.md
    review_v001.json

  reviews/
    human_review_log.md

  reports/
    production_log.md
    production_package.md
```

后续接入真实视频生成后，仍然不要新增独立 `generated-assets/` 根目录；资产参考图和完整分镜图片统一放在 assets 阶段下：

  assets/

  audio/
    narration.wav
    bgm.mp3
    sfx/

  edit/
    edit_manifest_v001.json
    rough_cut_v001.mp4

```

# Video Producer Skill

## 1. 使用场景

当用户希望开启、继续或推进一次视频生成任务时，使用本 Skill。

典型触发语：

- “创建一个新的视频项目”
- “开始一个视频生成会话”
- “继续 cyber_cat_trailer_20260429”
- “生成剧本 / 进入分镜 / 规划资产”
- “批准 brief / 修改剧本 / 重新生成分镜”
- “查看当前视频项目进度”

本 Skill 是视频生成流程的主控。制片人 Agent 不直接承担所有创作工作，而是负责调度、审核、保存和推进。

---

## 2. 核心原则

1. 所有视频 Agent 相关内容必须保存在 `video-agent/` 下。
2. 每次视频生成会话必须有独立会话目录。
3. 每次会话必须维护 `production_state.json`。
4. 每个阶段必须文件化保存产物。
5. 每个阶段必须先自动审核，再人工审核。
6. 未获得人工确认前，禁止进入下一阶段。
7. 上游产物变更后，所有依赖它的下游产物必须标记为 `stale`。
8. 子 Skill 只负责生成或审核；人工交互由制片人 Agent 统一负责。
9. 所有阶段变更必须写入 `production_state.json.history`。
10. 所有人工决策必须写入 `reviews/human_review_log.md` 和 `production_state.json.human_approvals`。

---

## 3. 目录约定

视频 Agent 根目录：

```text
video-agent/
```

会话根目录采用“主题目录 + 剧本/集数 session 目录”的两层结构：

```text
video-agent/video-generation-session/{topic_name}/{session_id}/
```

`session_id` 推荐格式：

```text
{topic_name}_no{episode_number}_{yyyymmdd}
```

示例：

```text
video-agent/video-generation-session/cyber_cat_trailer/cyber_cat_trailer_no1_20260503/
video-agent/video-generation-session/cyber_cat_trailer/cyber_cat_trailer_no2_20260506/
```

含义：`cyber_cat_trailer/` 是主题/系列目录；`no1`、`no2` 表示同一主题下第 1 段、第 2 段剧本或第 1 集、第 2 集。旧版扁平目录 `{session_name}_{yyyymmdd}` 仍作为 legacy session 可恢复，但新建主题和续写默认使用两层结构。

单次会话目录结构：

```text
{session_root}/
  production_state.json

  user_assets/
    user_assets_v001.md
    user_assets_v001.json
    uploads/
    transcripts/
    metadata/
    derived/

  brief/
    user_brief.md
    normalized_brief.json
    review_v001.md
    review_v001.json

  script/
    script_v001.md
    script_v001.json
    review_v001.md
    review_v001.json

  storyboard/
    storyboard_v001.md
    storyboard_v001.json
    storyboard_v001.csv
    review_v001.md
    review_v001.json

  assets/
    assets_manifest_v001.md
    assets_manifest_v001.json
    generated/v001/
    storyboard_frames/v001/
    review_v001.md
    review_v001.json

  reviews/
    human_review_log.md

  reports/
    production_log.md
    production_package.md
```

MVP 阶段只推进到 `production_package.md`。后续真实视频生成阶段再启用 `shots/`、`audio/`、`edit/` 等目录。

---

## 4. 新建视频生成会话

### 4.1 必要输入

新建会话时，用户必须先提供：

```text
主题名称
```

如果是同一主题的第一段剧本，默认 `episode_number = 1`；如果是剧情续写，默认在同一主题目录下创建下一个编号，例如 `no2`。如果无法判断当前应使用哪个编号，必须先询问用户，不要自行覆盖已有 session。

主题名称需要拆成两个字段：

- `display_name`：给人看的名称，可以是中文。
- `topic_name`：用于主题目录和 session_id，建议英文小写、数字、下划线、短横线。

如果用户只给中文名称，制片人 Agent 可以建议一个英文 `topic_name`，并让用户确认；如果用户明确接受自动命名，可直接使用该建议。

### 4.2 topic_name 规范化

规范化规则：

1. 转为小写。
2. 空格替换为下划线。
3. 删除不适合文件名的特殊字符。
4. 只保留英文、数字、下划线、短横线。
5. 如果无法从中文稳定生成英文名，询问用户提供英文主题名。

### 4.3 生成 session_id

使用 episode number 和当前日期 `yyyymmdd`：

```text
session_id = {topic_name}_no{episode_number}_{yyyymmdd}
```

例如：

```text
cyber_cat_trailer_no1_20260503
cyber_cat_trailer_no2_20260506
```

### 4.4 创建目录

创建：

```text
video-agent/video-generation-session/{topic_name}/{session_id}/
```

并创建子目录：

```text
user_assets/
brief/
script/
storyboard/
assets/
reviews/
reports/
```

### 4.5 捕获用户原始素材

在生成 brief 前，制片人 Agent 必须把本次 WebUI/API/CLI 输入中的所有原始信息保存到：

```text
{session_root}/user_assets/
```

必须生成：

```text
{session_root}/user_assets/user_assets_v001.md
{session_root}/user_assets/user_assets_v001.json
```

并按素材类型保存上传文件或派生文本：

```text
{session_root}/user_assets/uploads/       # 用户上传的原始图片、音频、视频、文档等；不得只保留临时 URL
{session_root}/user_assets/transcripts/   # 语音/视频转写文本，若当前工具无法转写则记录为 pending
{session_root}/user_assets/metadata/      # 每个上传文件的 mime、size、sha256、来源说明等 sidecar JSON
{session_root}/user_assets/derived/       # 图片/视频画面摘要、OCR、人工描述等派生说明
```

`user_assets_v001.json` 必须符合：

```text
video-agent/schemas/user-assets.schema.json
```

约束：

1. 文本剧本描述、补充要求、用户口述转写都要写入 `text_inputs[]` 或 `derived_notes[]`。
2. 每个上传文件必须有一个 `uploaded_assets[]` 记录，并使用 session-relative `stored_path`，例如 `user_assets/uploads/user_assets_001_reference.png`。
3. 如果当前运行环境无法直接读取某个 WebUI 上传文件，必须在 manifest 中记录原始文件名、用户描述、`stored_path = "user_assets/uploads/pending_<asset_id>_<filename>"`，并在 Markdown 里标记 `pending_file_copy`，不得假装已保存。
4. 后续 `brief/user_brief.md` 和 `brief/normalized_brief.json` 可以摘要引用 user_assets 内容，但不要把大文件或完整转写只放在 brief 中。
5. script、storyboard、assets、shots 阶段需要用户上传素材时，优先读取 `user_assets/user_assets_v{N}.json` 和其中的 session-relative 路径。

### 4.6 初始化 production_state.json

读取模板：

```text
video-agent/templates/production-state-template.json
```

替换占位符：

- `{{session_id}}`
- `{{display_name}}`
- `{{session_name}}`
- `{{yyyymmdd}}`
- `{{created_at_iso}}`
- `{{updated_at_iso}}`

写入：

```text
{session_root}/production_state.json
```

然后使用 schema 校验：

```text
video-agent/schemas/production-state.schema.json
```

校验失败必须停止流程，先修复状态文件。

### 4.7 初始化日志文件

```

# Video Agent File Workflow

## Class of task

Use this skill when a user wants Hermes to act as a video generation producer/orchestrator for a file-based, human-in-the-loop creative pipeline: create or resume a session, generate stage artifacts, run automated review, stop for human approval, and validate machine-readable outputs.

## Trigger phrases

Load this skill when the user asks to:

- “作为视频生成 Agent 制片人主控工作”
- “创建新视频生成会话”
- “继续 {session_id}” for a video session
- “执行到 brief/script/storyboard/assets 阶段”
- “批准 brief/script/storyboard/assets/shots，进入下一阶段”
- “接入视频片段生成 skill / Doubao Seedance / 火山方舟视频生成接口”
- “根据 storyboard 和 assets 组织视频生成 API 调用参数”
- “提交视频生成任务 / 提交 Doubao Seedance 任务 / 逐个 shot 生成”
- “使用即梦 CLI / dreamina CLI / 剪映即梦生成视频片段”
- “把 Dreamina CLI / 即梦官方 skill 安装到 video-agent 里”
- “一个 shot 生成完并确认效果后再继续下一个 shot”
- “跳过后面的 shots，先用已经生成的 clips 测试混剪 / edit skill”
- “用部分已生成 shot 做 rough cut / partial edit test”
- “验证第一个 demo 是否跑通”
- “检查 production_state.json / review gate / stale 产物”
- “业务用户看不懂太多 JSON/英文/文件，想优化可视化体验”
- “希望在当前对话框中直接看到剧本、分镜表、分镜图、视频片段”
- “给 video-agent 做业务审核面板 / dashboard / current_review”
- “把剧本、分镜、资产、shots、视频效果审核改成中文卡片/表格展示”

## Project location

For the user's Hermes project, the video agent workspace is:

```text
/Users/edy/vedio-agent/hermes-agent-main/video-agent/
```

All video-agent artifacts must remain under that workspace. The recommended per-session root now uses a two-level topic/session layout:

```text
video-agent/video-generation-session/{topic_name}/{session_id}/
```

`session_id` format:

```text
{topic_name}_no{episode_number}_{yyyymmdd}
```

Example:

```text
video-agent/video-generation-session/cyber_cat_trailer/cyber_cat_trailer_no1_20260503/
video-agent/video-generation-session/cyber_cat_trailer/cyber_cat_trailer_no2_20260506/
```

Legacy flat sessions such as `video-agent/video-generation-session/cyber_cat_trailer_20260503/` remain recoverable, but new themes and sequels should default to the two-level layout.

## Required files to read first

Before creating, resuming, or advancing a video session, read the current project-local source of truth:

```text
video-agent/README.md
video-agent/skills/video-producer/SKILL.md
```

For stage execution, also read the relevant schemas/templates/reviewer docs:

```text
video-agent/templates/production-state-template.json
video-agent/templates/user-brief-template.md
video-agent/schemas/production-state.schema.json
video-agent/schemas/brief.schema.json
video-agent/schemas/script.schema.json
video-agent/schemas/storyboard.schema.json
video-agent/schemas/assets-manifest.schema.json
video-agent/schemas/shot-generation-tasks.schema.json
video-agent/schemas/shot-submission.schema.json
video-agent/schemas/dreamina-shot-submission.schema.json
video-agent/schemas/edit-manifest.schema.json
video-agent/schemas/review.schema.json
video-agent/skills/mock-scriptwriter/SKILL.md
video-agent/skills/mock-storyboard/SKILL.md
video-agent/skills/mock-asset-planner/SKILL.md
video-agent/skills/doubao-seedance-shot-generator/SKILL.md
video-agent/skills/doubao-seedance-shot-submitter/SKILL.md
video-agent/skills/dreamina-shot-submitter/SKILL.md
video-agent/skills/simple-video-editor/SKILL.md
video-agent/skills/reviewers/*.md
```

Only read stage-specific files as needed to avoid unnecessary context, but always read README and video-producer first.

## Core workflow rules

1. The producer agent owns all user interaction; subskills/reviewers do not ask the user directly.
2. Every session has one `production_state.json` as the source of truth.
3. Every key stage follows:

```text
generate artifact -> automated review -> human review -> approve/revise -> next stage
```

4. Never enter the next stage before human approval.
5. If an upstream artifact changes, mark downstream artifacts as `stale` and do not use stale artifacts to advance.
6. Save human-readable artifacts as Markdown/CSV and machine-readable artifacts as JSON.
7. Validate all JSON outputs against their schemas before asking for human approval.
8. Automated review can request human review only when:

```text
verdict = pass
blocking_issues = []
```

## Creating a new session

When the user provides a topic name and request:

1. Normalize the topic name into `topic_name` using lowercase English, numbers, `_`, and `-`.
2. Determine the episode number:
   - for a new theme, default to `no1`;
   - for a sequel/continuation under the same theme, use the next available number such as `no2`;
   - if the next number is ambiguous, ask the user instead of overwriting an existing session.
3. Use the current date from the system (`date +%Y%m%d`) to build `session_id = {topic_name}_no{episode_number}_{yyyymmdd}`.
4. The session root should be `video-agent/video-generation-session/{topic_name}/{session_id}/`.
5. Check whether the session directory exists and is non-empty; do not overwrite silently.
6. Create directories:

```text
brief/
script/
storyboard/
assets/
reviews/
reports/
```

7. Initialize `production_state.json`.
8. Create `reviews/human_review_log.md` and `reports/production_log.md`.
9. Generate only the requested stage; if the user says “只执行到 brief 阶段”, stop after brief review and human-review request.

## Brief stage checklist

Expected artifacts:

```text
production_state.json
brief/user_brief.md
brief/normalized_brief.json
brief/review_v001.md
brief/review_v001.json
reviews/human_review_log.md
reports/production_log.md
```

Validation:

- `production_state.json` parses and validates against `production-state.schema.json`.
- `brief/normalized_brief.json` parses and validates against `brief.schema.json`.
- `brief/review_v001.json` parses and validates against `review.schema.json`.
- `production_state.json.current_stage == "brief"`.
- `stages.brief.status == "pending_human_review"` after review passes.
- `script`, `storyboard`, `assets`, and `package` remain `not_started`.
- `review_v001.json.verdict == "pass"` and `blocking_issues == []` before asking the user to approve.

## Storyboard stage checklist

When the user approves script and asks to enter storyboard:

1. Re-read `production_state.json`, the approved script JSON, the script review JSON, `storyboard.schema.json`, `review.schema.json`, `mock-storyboard/SKILL.md`, and `storyboard-reviewer.md`.
2. Confirm the script stage is currently `pending_human_review`, its automated review is `verdict=pass` with `blocking_issues=[]`, and the brief stage is already `approved`.
3. Record exactly the script approval in both `production_state.json.human_approvals` and `reviews/human_review_log.md`; do not duplicate earlier brief approvals.
4. Generate:

```text
storyboard/storyboard_v001.md
storyboard/storyboard_v001.json
storyboard/storyboard_v001.csv
storyboard/review_v001.md
storyboard/review_v001.json
```

5. The storyboard should cover every script scene and beat, keep `actual_duration_sec` within `duration_tolerance_sec`, include `asset_requirements` and generation/negative prompts for every shot, and preserve continuity constraints from the approved script.
6. If an upstream reviewer gave a non-blocking quality suggestion, incorporate it in storyboard design where possible. Example: if a future hook has too much text, make it a brief visual flicker or image beat rather than an explanatory subtitle.
7. Validate `production_state.json`, `storyboard_v001.json`, and `storyboard/review_v001.json` against their schemas; also verify scene/beat references and CSV/JSON consistency.
8. Stop at `stages.storyboard.status == "pending_human_review"`; leave `assets`, `shots`, `edit`, and `package` as `not_started` unless the user explicitly approves storyboard later.
9. Read back `storyboard_v001.md`, `storyboard/review_v001.md`, and the relevant `production_state.json` lines before responding.

## Assets stage checklist

When the user approves storyboard and asks to enter assets:

1. Re-read `production_state.json`, the approved storyboard JSON, the storyboard review JSON, the approved script JSON, `assets-manifest.schema.json`, `review.schema.json`, `mock-asset-planner/SKILL.md`, and `asset-reviewer.md`.
2. Confirm the storyboard stage is currently `pending_human_review`, its review gate is `verdict=pass` with `blocking_issues=[]`, and the script stage is already `approved`.
3. Record exactly the stage being approved now. Do not duplicate approvals for earlier stages that are already approved; if a preserved todo or handoff mentions an older approval again, treat it as stale task-list wording and continue from the actual `production_state.json`.
4. Generate:

```text
assets/assets_manifest_v001.md
assets/assets_manifest_v001.json
assets/review_v001.md
assets/review_v001.json
```

5. The assets manifest should cover all script characters, all script scenes, every storyboard shot, and every `storyboard.shots[].asset_requirements[]` item. `coverage.unresolved_asset_requirements` must be empty before requesting human review.
6. Preserve continuity constraints as asset-level rules. If a prior revision distinguished a clue/projection from a physical object, represent them as separate assets so downstream generators cannot reuse the wrong one in early scenes.
7. Validate `production_state.json`, `assets_manifest_v001.json`, and `assets/review_v001.json` against their schemas.
8. Stop at `stages.assets.status == "pending_human_review"`; leave `package`, `shots`, and `edit` as `not_started` unless the user explicitly approves assets later.
9. Read back `assets_manifest_v001.md`, `assets/review_v001.md`, and the relevant `production_state.json` lines before responding.

## Shots request-planning checklist

When the user has an approved storyboard and assets manifest and asks to connect a video clip generation API/skill (for example Doubao Seedance / 火山方舟), treat this as a `shots` request-planning stage before any real submission:

1. Re-read `production_state.json`, approved `storyboard/storyboard_v{N}.json`, approved `assets/assets_manifest_v{N}.json`, `shot-generation-tasks.schema.json`, `review.schema.json`, the shot generator skill doc, and the shot-task reviewer doc.
2. Confirm `stages.storyboard.status == "approved"`, `stages.assets.status == "approved"`, both are not stale, and the versions match `production_state.json.versions`.
3. Generate request-package artifacts without calling the external API:

```text
shots/shot_tasks_v001.md
shots/shot_tasks_v001.json
shots/review_v001.md
shots/review_v001.json
```

4. For Doubao Seedance request packages, preserve the provider contract in JSON: endpoint, model, `content` array, text prompt, optional reference image/video/audio items, `generate_audio`, `ratio`, `duration`, and `watermark`.
5. Never write real credentials into artifacts. Use `Bearer ${ARK_API_KEY}` or another explicit environment-variable placeholder in headers/curl templates, and read the real value only in a later submitter/executor stage.
6. Keep the shot generator's default responsibility narrow: it organizes API parameters and curl/request templates; it does not submit jobs unless the user explicitly asks for a submitter/execution stage.
7. Validate `shot_tasks_v001.json` against `shot-generation-tasks.schema.json` and validate the shot review JSON against `review.schema.json`.
8. Ensure coverage is complete: every storyboard shot has one task, `coverage.missing_shot_ids` is empty, and all referenced asset keys exist in the assets manifest or are explicitly marked unresolved.
9. Stop at `stages.shots.status == "pending_human_review"` if the review gate passes. Ask the user to approve shots before submitting real video-generation jobs.
10. If a production package was already generated before inserting or regenerating the shots stage, mark the package `stale` with a clear reason so the old package is not treated as final.

## Sequential shot submission checklist

When the user approves a shot request package and asks to submit video generation jobs, treat this as a sequential execution subflow under the `shots` stage:

1. Re-read `production_state.json`, `shots/shot_tasks_v{N}.json`, `shots/review_v{N}.json`, `shot-submission.schema.json`, and the shot submitter skill doc.
2. Confirm the request package review gate is `verdict=pass` and `blocking_issues=[]`, and record the user's approval for `shots v{N}` in both `production_state.json.human_approvals` and `reviews/human_review_log.md`.
3. Enforce `one_shot_at_a_time`: submit only the lowest-order shot not already human-approved. Never submit shot N+1 while shot N is submitted, pending generation, pending human effect review, failed, or rejected.
4. Read credentials only from the runtime environment, e.g. `ARK_API_KEY`. Never write the token into JSON, Markdown, curl templates, logs, or final responses.
5. Write provider responses under:

```text
shots/provider_responses/{shot_id}_submit_response_v{N}.json
```

6. Track the sequential queue in:

```

# Mock Scriptwriter Skill

虽然目录名仍为 `mock-scriptwriter`，本 Skill 当前承担 video-agent 项目的 production scriptwriter 职责；`mock` 只表示它不直接调用外部视频/图片生成器，不表示低质量输出或只跑通流程。

## 1. 使用场景

当制片人 Agent 已经获得用户批准的 `brief/normalized_brief.json`，并需要生成 MVP 阶段可供分镜 Skill 使用的剧本产物时，使用本 Skill。

本 Skill 的目标是稳定输出下游可解析、可分镜、可审核的剧本结构，帮助跑通：

```text
user_assets -> brief -> script -> storyboard -> assets -> shots -> edit -> package -> package_complete
```

---

## 2. 输入契约

### 2.1 创作质量参考与策略参数包注入

本 Skill 仍然只有一个输出契约：生成当前项目要求的 `script/script_v{N}.md` 和 `script/script_v{N}.json`。不同视频类型的差异不通过改变输出格式实现，而通过读取剧本策略参数包注入。

必须参考共享原则：

```text
{video_agent_root}/skills/mock-scriptwriter/references/shared-script-principles.md
```

并根据 brief 中已有信息选择一个策略参数包。选择依据只能来自当前 schema 已允许的字段，例如：

```text
brief/normalized_brief.json:
- topic
- platform
- audience
- style.summary
- style.keywords
- narrative_requirements.genre
- narrative_requirements.tone
- narrative_requirements.structure
- narrative_requirements.must_include
- narrative_requirements.must_avoid
- content_constraints.must_include
- content_constraints.must_avoid
```

可用剧本策略参数包：

```text
{video_agent_root}/skills/mock-scriptwriter/references/script-strategy-packs/brand-video.md
{video_agent_root}/skills/mock-scriptwriter/references/script-strategy-packs/product-ad.md
{video_agent_root}/skills/mock-scriptwriter/references/script-strategy-packs/social-short-video.md
{video_agent_root}/skills/mock-scriptwriter/references/script-strategy-packs/launch-video.md
{video_agent_root}/skills/mock-scriptwriter/references/script-strategy-packs/recruitment-video.md
{video_agent_root}/skills/mock-scriptwriter/references/script-strategy-packs/investment-promo.md
```

参数包只影响创作策略，不改变本 Skill 定义的输出路径、文件命名、JSON 字段、版本号、review gate、human approval、stale 规则或 `production_state.json` 更新方式。

如果参数包与本 Skill、`video-agent/README.md`、`skills/video-producer/SKILL.md`、`schemas/script.schema.json` 冲突，以当前项目规范为准。

用户只要求“写剧本”时，只生成 script 阶段产物，不同时生成 storyboard。

必须读取：

```text
{session_root}/user_assets/user_assets_v{N}.json
{session_root}/brief/normalized_brief.json
```

该文件必须符合：

```text
video-agent/schemas/user-assets.schema.json
video-agent/schemas/brief.schema.json
```

必要字段：

- session_id
- display_name
- original_request
- topic
- core_idea
- target_duration_sec
- aspect_ratio
- language
- platform
- audience
- style
- narrative_requirements
- visual_requirements
- audio_requirements
- content_constraints

如果 user_assets 或 brief 缺失，或 brief 未通过人工审核，不能生成剧本，应返回给制片人 Agent 处理。

生成剧本时，如果 `user_assets_v{N}.json` 中存在用户上传的参考图片、语音转写、视频摘要、文档或 derived notes，必须把它们当作 brief 的上游原始证据使用；引用路径必须保持 session-relative，不要引用临时上传 URL。

---

## 3. 输出契约

输出目录：

```text
{session_root}/script/
```

必须生成：

```text
{session_root}/script/script_v{N}.md
{session_root}/script/script_v{N}.json
```

MVP 初次生成使用：

```text
script_v001.md
script_v001.json
```

返工时递增版本号：

```text
script_v002.md
script_v002.json
```

`script_v{N}.json` 必须符合：

```text
video-agent/schemas/script.schema.json
```

---

## 4. JSON 输出结构

`script_v{N}.json` 的字段、类型、必填项和 ID 格式必须完全遵守：

```text
video-agent/schemas/script.schema.json
```

本 Skill 不在文档中内嵌具体题材示例，避免示例世界观、角色、题材词污染真实项目。

必须包含：

- 至少 1 个 character。
- 至少 1 个 scene。
- 至少 6 个 beat；如果目标时长 >= 60 秒，建议 9 个 beat。
- 每个 scene 必须引用已有 character_id。
- 每个 beat 必须引用已有 scene_id。
- narration_plan.segments 需要引用已有 beat_id。

---

## 5. Markdown 输出结构

`script_v{N}.md` 应给人阅读，推荐结构：

```markdown
# {title}

## Logline

{logline}

## 项目信息

- Session ID: {session_id}
- Version: {version}
- Target Duration: {target_duration_sec}s
- Aspect Ratio: {aspect_ratio}
- Language: {language}

## 主要角色

- **{name}** ({character_id}): {canonical_appearance}。{role}

## 场景 1 - {heading}

{visual_description}

### Beats

1. **{beat_id}**: {description}
   - Visual turning point: {visual_turning_point}
   - Voiceover/Dialog: {dialogue_or_voiceover}

## 旁白计划

- {segment_id} / {beat_id}: {text}

## 连续性说明

- {continuity_note}
```

---

## 6. 剧本生成策略

mock-scriptwriter 应根据 brief 生成“结构稳定、类型正确、可分镜”的剧本：

1. 使用 brief.topic 作为主题。
2. 使用 brief.core_idea 扩展 logline。
3. 使用 brief.target_duration_sec 设置目标时长。
4. 使用 brief.aspect_ratio 和 brief.language 保持下游一致。
5. 根据 brief 中的 topic、platform、audience、style、narrative_requirements、content_constraints 选择 strategy pack。
6. 剧本结构来自匹配的 strategy pack；不要硬套单一剧情结构。
7. 如果没有匹配 strategy pack，才使用 generic structure：处境建立 -> 问题显影 -> 解决/变化 -> 结果证明 -> 收束。
8. beat 数量根据目标时长动态决定：
   - 15-30 秒：4-7 个 beats。
   - 30-60 秒：6-9 个 beats。
   - 60-120 秒：8-12 个 beats。
9. 每个 beat 必须有 `visual_turning_point`，且能被 storyboard 阶段转成镜头。
10. 禁止沿用任何示例世界观、角色、题材词；只使用当前 brief 中明确存在的信息。

---

## 7. 推荐输出组织

剧本组织应服务于当前视频类型，而不是固定套用某一种剧情结构：

- 产品广告：痛点 -> 产品早出现 -> 使用动作 -> 证据/反应 -> packshot/CTA。
- 品牌片：真实处境 -> 价值显影 -> 能力被结果感知 -> 情绪收束。
- 社媒短视频：处境/焦虑 -> 踩坑 -> 发现 -> 验证 -> 用户口吻推荐。
- 发布视频：旧工作流摩擦 -> 产品出现 -> demo beats -> wow moment -> 清晰收束。
- 招聘片：真实项目/数字 -> 员工具体工作 -> 协作现场 -> 成长/选择理由。
- 招商/融资片：为什么现在 -> 问题规模 -> 解决方案 -> 牵引力 -> 交付能力 -> ask。

无论哪种类型，都必须保证：

- characters / scenes / beats 满足 schema。
- 每个 scene 有明确 location、time、visual_description。
- 每个 beat 有可拍动作、信息落点和 visual_turning_point。
- 旁白、字幕、画面分工，不要完全同字重复。

---

## 8. 自动审核前自检

生成后必须自检：

- [ ] `script_v{N}.md` 已保存。

```

> TOOL

# Mock Storyboard Skill

虽然目录名仍为 `mock-storyboard`，本 Skill 当前承担 video-agent 项目的 production storyboard 职责；`mock` 只表示它不直接提交外部视频生成任务，不表示低质量输出或只跑通流程。

## 1. 使用场景

当制片人 Agent 已经获得用户批准的剧本 `script/script_v{N}.json`，并需要将剧本转换成可执行的分镜表时，使用本 Skill。

本 Skill 的目标是稳定输出下游可解析、可审核、可执行生成的分镜结构。

目标链路：

```text
user_assets -> brief -> script -> storyboard -> assets -> shots -> edit -> package -> package_complete
```

---

## 2. 输入契约

### 2.1 创作质量参考与风格参数包注入

本 Skill 仍然只有一个输出契约：生成当前项目要求的 `storyboard/storyboard_v{N}.md`、`storyboard/storyboard_v{N}.json` 和 `storyboard/storyboard_v{N}.csv`。不同视频类型的视觉差异不通过改变输出格式实现，而通过读取分镜风格参数包注入。

必须参考共享原则：

```text
{video_agent_root}/skills/mock-storyboard/references/shared-storyboard-principles.md
```

并根据 brief/script 中已有信息选择一个风格参数包。选择依据只能来自当前 schema 已允许的字段，例如：

```text
brief/normalized_brief.json:
- topic
- platform
- audience
- style.summary
- style.keywords
- visual_requirements.format
- visual_requirements.color_palette
- visual_requirements.camera_style
- visual_requirements.character_style
- visual_requirements.scene_style
- narrative_requirements.genre
- narrative_requirements.tone
- content_constraints.must_avoid

script/script_v{N}.json:
- title
- logline
- scenes[].visual_description
- beats[].description
- beats[].visual_turning_point
- narration_plan.style
```

可用分镜风格参数包：

```text
{video_agent_root}/skills/mock-storyboard/references/storyboard-style-packs/brand-video.md
{video_agent_root}/skills/mock-storyboard/references/storyboard-style-packs/product-ad.md
{video_agent_root}/skills/mock-storyboard/references/storyboard-style-packs/social-short-video.md
{video_agent_root}/skills/mock-storyboard/references/storyboard-style-packs/launch-video.md
{video_agent_root}/skills/mock-storyboard/references/storyboard-style-packs/recruitment-video.md
{video_agent_root}/skills/mock-storyboard/references/storyboard-style-packs/investment-promo.md
```

参数包只影响视觉风格、镜头语言、字幕策略和 AI 生成提示质量，不改变本 Skill 定义的输出路径、文件命名、JSON/CSV 字段、版本号、review gate、human approval、stale 规则或 `production_state.json` 更新方式。

如果参数包与本 Skill、`video-agent/README.md`、`skills/video-producer/SKILL.md`、`schemas/storyboard.schema.json` 冲突，以当前项目规范为准。

禁止在 storyboard 阶段直接提交 Dreamina / Seedance 视频生成任务；视频生成必须等 storyboard 和 assets 均通过审核后，由 shots 阶段处理。

必须读取：

```text
{session_root}/user_assets/user_assets_v{N}.json
{session_root}/brief/normalized_brief.json
{session_root}/script/script_v{N}.json
```

输入文件必须符合：

```text
video-agent/schemas/user-assets.schema.json
video-agent/schemas/brief.schema.json
video-agent/schemas/script.schema.json
```

制片人 Agent 必须确认：

- user_assets 阶段已 generated，且 `user_assets/user_assets_v{N}.json` 可解析。
- brief 阶段已 approved。
- script 阶段已 approved。
- script 阶段未 stale。

如果 user_assets 缺失、script 未通过人工审核，不能生成分镜。

生成分镜时，如果 `user_assets_v{N}.json` 中存在参考图片、视频摘要、OCR、语音转写或其他 derived notes，必须作为画面、风格、角色、场景和动作设计的上游参考；引用路径必须保持 session-relative，不要引用临时上传 URL。

---

## 3. 输出契约

输出目录：

```text
{session_root}/storyboard/
```

必须生成：

```text
{session_root}/storyboard/storyboard_v{N}.md
{session_root}/storyboard/storyboard_v{N}.json
{session_root}/storyboard/storyboard_v{N}.csv
```

初次生成使用：

```text
storyboard_v001.md
storyboard_v001.json
storyboard_v001.csv
```

返工时递增版本号：

```text
storyboard_v002.md
storyboard_v002.json
storyboard_v002.csv
```

`storyboard_v{N}.json` 必须符合：

```text
video-agent/schemas/storyboard.schema.json
```

---

## 4. JSON 输出结构

`storyboard_v{N}.json` 的字段、类型、必填项和 ID 格式必须完全遵守：

```text
video-agent/schemas/storyboard.schema.json
```

本 Skill 不在文档中内嵌具体题材示例，避免示例世界观、角色、场景、镜头词污染真实项目。

每个 shot 必须包含：

- shot_id
- scene_id
- beat_ids
- order
- duration_sec
- shot_size
- camera_angle
- camera_movement
- scene
- visual
- character_action
- dialogue_or_voiceover
- sound.ambience
- sound.sfx
- sound.foley
- sound.music_cue
- transition
- aspect_ratio
- asset_requirements
- generation_prompt
- negative_prompt
- notes

---

## 5. Markdown 输出结构

`storyboard_v{N}.md` 给人审阅读，推荐结构：

```markdown
# {title} 分镜表 {version}

## 项目信息

- Session ID: {session_id}
- Source Script: {source_script_version}
- Target Duration: {target_duration_sec}s
- Actual Duration: {actual_duration_sec}s
- Aspect Ratio: {aspect_ratio}

## 分镜表

| Shot | 时长 | 景别 | 视角 | 运镜 | 场景 | 画面内容 | 旁白/对白 | 音效 | 转场 |
|---|---:|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| shot_001 | 6s | {shot_size} | {camera_angle} | {camera_movement} | {scene_location} | ... | ... | ... | cut |

## 覆盖检查

- Covered scenes: ...
- Covered beats: ...
- Uncovered scenes: ...
- Uncovered beats: ...
```

---

## 6. CSV 输出结构

`storyboard_v{N}.csv` 必须包含表头：

```csv
shot_id,scene_id,beat_ids,order,duration_sec,shot_size,camera_angle,camera_movement,scene,visual,character_action,dialogue_or_voiceover,sound_ambience,sound_sfx,sound_foley,sound_music_cue,transition,aspect_ratio,asset_requirements,generation_prompt,negative_prompt,notes
```

其中：

- `beat_ids` 多值用 `|` 分隔。
- `asset_requirements` 多值用 `|` 分隔，建议写 asset_key。
- CSV 内容必须与 JSON 保持一致。

---

## 7. Mock 生成策略

mock-storyboard 应从 script 的 scenes 和 beats 生成 shots：

1. 每个 beat 至少被一个 shot 覆盖，除非明确记录到 uncovered_beat_ids。
2. 每个 scene 至少被一个 shot 覆盖，除非明确记录到 uncovered_scene_ids。
3. shot 顺序必须与 beat.order 基本一致。
4. 总时长 actual_duration_sec 应接近 target_duration_sec。
5. MVP 默认允许误差：target_duration_sec 的 10%，最少 3 秒。
6. shot 数根据 target_duration_sec、content_type、style pack、节奏需求动态决定。
7. 常规 AI 视频生成镜头建议 ≥ 3 秒；不要为了“快”把镜头切到不可生成、不可读。
8. 标准参考：
   - 15-30 秒：4-8 个 shots。
   - 30-60 秒：6-12 个 shots。
   - 60-120 秒：10-20 个 shots。
9. 如果 style pack 明确允许 micro-montage，才允许更短的内部快切；必须标注用途，且每个短镜头只承担一个视觉功能。
10. 每个 shot 都必须有资产需求 asset_requirements。
11. 每个 shot 都必须给视频生成阶段预留 generation_prompt 和 negative_prompt。
12. 每个 shot 必须写清 visual function、action chain、cut point，避免只写结果摘要。

---

创建：

## 5. 恢复视频生成会话

当用户说：

```text
继续 {session_id}
```

制片人 Agent 必须：

1. 查找目录：
   `video-agent/video-generation-session/{session_id}/`
2. 读取：
   `{session_root}/production_state.json`
3. 使用 `production-state.schema.json` 校验状态文件。
4. 根据 `current_stage` 和 `stages` 判断下一步。
5. 向用户汇报当前进度、当前待审核/待生成阶段和关键产物路径。

如果找不到目录或状态文件，不要猜测，应提示用户可用的会话目录或要求用户重新提供 session_id。

---

## 6. 阶段状态机

合法阶段：

```text
user_assets
brief
script
storyboard
assets
shots
edit
package
package_complete
```

MVP 阶段实际推进：

```text
user_assets -> brief -> script -> storyboard -> assets -> package -> package_complete
```

接入真实视频片段生成后，扩展推进：

```text
user_assets -> brief -> script -> storyboard -> assets -> shots -> edit -> package -> package_complete
```

合法状态：

```text
not_started
in_progress
generated
auto_review_passed
auto_review_failed
pending_human_review
approved
rejected
stale
complete
```

### 6.1 标准阶段推进

每个阶段必须遵循：

```text
not_started
-> in_progress
-> generated
-> auto_review_passed 或 auto_review_failed
-> pending_human_review
-> approved 或 rejected
```

只有 `approved` 后，才能进入下一阶段。

### 6.2 自动审核失败

如果自动审核失败：

1. 阶段状态设为 `auto_review_failed`。
2. 保存 `review_v{N}.md` 和 `review_v{N}.json`。
3. 不请求用户批准进入下一阶段。
4. 根据 blocking issues 调度对应子 Skill 返工。
5. 返工后生成新版本或覆盖当前草稿，推荐生成新版本。

### 6.3 人工审核拒绝

如果用户回复“修改：...”或明确拒绝：

1. 当前阶段状态设为 `rejected`。
2. 记录人工反馈。
3. 生成新版本，例如 `script_v002.json`。
4. 重新自动审核。
5. 再次请求人工审核。

### 6.4 人工审核批准

如果用户回复“批准”：

1. 当前阶段状态设为 `approved`。
2. 写入 `human_approvals`。
3. 写入 `reviews/human_review_log.md`。
4. 推进 `current_stage` 到下一阶段。
5. 开始下一阶段前，再次确认上游依赖没有 stale。

---

## 7. Stale 规则

当某个阶段重新生成新版本时，所有依赖它的下游阶段必须标记为 `stale`，除非它们尚未开始。

依赖关系：

```text
user_assets -> brief -> script -> storyboard -> assets -> package
user_assets -> brief -> storyboard
script -> assets
storyboard -> assets
assets -> shots
shots -> edit
edit -> package
assets -> package
```

MVP 中可使用 production_state 里的 `depends_on` 字段进行判断。

示例：

如果 `script` 从 `v001` 改为 `v002`：

- `storyboard` 如果已有产物，设为 `stale`。
- `assets` 如果已有产物，设为 `stale`。
- `package` 如果已有产物，设为 `stale`。

同时写入：

```json
"stale_reason": "script updated from v001 to v002"
```

禁止使用 stale 产物推进下一阶段。

---

{session_root}/storyboard/storyboard_v{N}.json

```

### 8.5 shot submitter / video generation

用户批准 `shots/shot_tasks_v{N}.json` 后，才进入真实提交。当前推荐 Dreamina CLI 路线：

video-agent/skills/dreamina-cli/SKILL.md
```

规则：

1. 一次只提交一个 shot。
2. 当前 shot 未生成完成、未人工确认效果前，禁止提交下一个 shot。
3. 每次真实 Dreamina 提交前确认 `model_version`，默认 `seedance2.0fast_vip`。
4. Generator Command 根据任务自动选择，不固定写死 `text2video`。
5. 提交和查询响应写入 `shots/dreamina_responses/`，下载文件写入 `shots/downloads/{shot_id}/`。

### 8.6 edit / mixdown

视频片段生成后调用：

```text
video-agent/skills/simple-video-editor/SKILL.md
```

职责：使用 ffmpeg 将已生成并通过当前测试门的视频片段混剪/拼接成 rough cut 或 final cut。

输入：

```text
{session_root}/shots/dreamina_submission_v{N}.json
{session_root}/shots/downloads/{shot_id}/*.mp4
```

输出：

```text
{session_root}/edit/edit_manifest_v{N}.md
{session_root}/edit/edit_manifest_v{N}.json
{session_root}/edit/rough_cut_v{N}.mp4
{session_root}/edit/review_v{N}.md
{session_root}/edit/review_v{N}.json
```

允许 partial edit test：如果用户明确要求跳过剩余 shots、先用已有 clips 测试混剪 Skill，必须记录 `shots/shot_generation_skip_v{N}.json`，并在 edit manifest/package 中标记 partial coverage。

鉴权规则：不得写入真实 token；curl/CLI 模板只能使用环境变量占位或本地 CLI 登录态，不得复制 credential。

## 9. 自动审核职责

每个阶段生成后，必须生成：

```text
{stage}/review_v{N}.md
{stage}/review_v{N}.json
```

统一 review JSON 结构：

```json
{
  "stage": "script",
  "version": "v001",
  "verdict": "pass",
  "score": 0.9,
  "blocking_issues": [],
  "non_blocking_issues": [],
  "approved_items": [],
  "suggestions": []
}
```

`verdict` 可取：

```text
pass
needs_revision
fail
```

只有 `verdict = pass` 且无 blocking issues 时，才可以进入人工审核。

每次请求人工审核时，制片人 Agent 必须展示：

1. 阶段名称。

# Asset Planner Skill

## 1. Overview

`asset-planner` is the Video Agent asset-stage skill. It sits after the upstream scriptwriter and storyboard skills:

```text
scriptwriter -> storyboard -> asset-planner -> asset-reviewer -> shot/video generation
```

Its job is not only to make a mock manifest. It must use the approved script and approved storyboard together to:

1. Extract reusable production assets: characters, character turnarounds, scenes/backgrounds, props, UI/FX cues, style references, visual systems, and shot-critical image needs.
2. Write a strict schema-valid asset manifest:
   ```text
   {session_root}/assets/assets_manifest_v{ASSETS_VERSION}.json
   {session_root}/assets/assets_manifest_v{ASSETS_VERSION}.md
   ```
3. Optionally generate actual reusable asset reference images and save them inside the current session assets folder.
4. Generate a complete set of cinematic storyboard frame images for every storyboard shot, using the script, storyboard, and generated asset references as continuity anchors.
5. Keep all asset-stage outputs inside:
   ```text
   {session_root}/assets/
   ```

The machine-readable asset manifest must validate against:

```text
video-agent/schemas/assets-manifest.schema.json
```

Do not use the old standalone image-storyboard project layout (`project_manifest.json`, `storyboard-images/`, `managed-photos/`, `deliverables/`) inside Video Agent sessions. In Video Agent, asset reference images and storyboard frame images both live under the session `assets/` tree.

## 2. When to Use

Use this skill when:

- The script stage and storyboard stage have both been approved.
- The user asks to extract assets from the script and storyboard.
- The user asks for character, scene, prop, visual-style, UI/FX, or frame reference images before video generation.
- The user asks to generate a full set of realistic cinematic storyboard images from the approved script/storyboard.
- A downstream shot/video generator needs `assets/assets_manifest_v{N}.json` plus local image references for consistent image-to-video or multiframe workflows.
- Script or storyboard changes make previous assets stale and the assets stage must be regenerated.

Do not use this skill for:

- Writing the script itself.
- Writing the storyboard table itself.
- Submitting video-generation jobs.
- Creating final edited videos.
- Adding non-schema fields into `assets_manifest_v{N}.json`.

## 3. Required Inputs

The producer or caller must provide a session root under:

```text
video-agent/video-generation-session/{topic_name}/{session_id}/
```

Required files:

```text
{session_root}/production_state.json
{session_root}/script/script_v{SCRIPT_VERSION}.json
{session_root}/script/script_v{SCRIPT_VERSION}.md
{session_root}/storyboard/storyboard_v{STORYBOARD_VERSION}.json
{session_root}/storyboard/storyboard_v{STORYBOARD_VERSION}.md
video-agent/schemas/script.schema.json
video-agent/schemas/storyboard.schema.json
video-agent/schemas/assets-manifest.schema.json
```

Before generating anything, verify:

- `production_state.json` exists and parses.
- `script` stage is `approved` and not `stale`.
- `storyboard` stage is `approved` and not `stale`.
- `script_v{N}.json` validates against `script.schema.json`.
- `storyboard_v{N}.json` validates against `storyboard.schema.json`.
- The script/storyboard versions match `production_state.json`.

If any prerequisite fails, stop and report the blocker. Do not create assets from stale or unapproved upstream files.

## 4. Output Contract and Paths

All outputs must stay under:

```text
{session_root}/assets/
```

### 4.1 Canonical schema outputs

Required for every asset-planner run:

```text
{session_root}/assets/assets_manifest_v{ASSETS_VERSION}.json
{session_root}/assets/assets_manifest_v{ASSETS_VERSION}.md
```

Initial version is `v001`; regeneration increments to `v002`, `v003`, etc.

### 4.2 Generated reusable asset image paths

The current `assets-manifest.schema.json` has `additionalProperties: false`. It does not define image path fields. Therefore, never add `image_path`, `local_path`, `url`, `file`, `generated_image`, or `reference_image` to `assets_manifest_v{N}.json`.

Actual reusable asset images must be copied into:

```text
{session_root}/assets/generated/{ASSETS_VERSION}/{asset_id}_{asset_key}.png
```

Examples:

```text
assets/generated/v001/asset_001_char_001_protagonist_turnaround.png
assets/generated/v001/asset_006_scene_001_rainy_alley_background.png
assets/generated/v001/asset_018_prop_001_phone_notification.png
```

Required operational sidecars for production image mode:

```text
{session_root}/assets/generated/assets_generation_queue_v{ASSETS_VERSION}.json
{session_root}/assets/generated/assets_generation_status_v{ASSETS_VERSION}.json
```

Optional convenience output:

```text
{session_root}/assets/generated/contact_sheet_assets_v{ASSETS_VERSION}.png
```

These sidecars are the canonical machine-readable place for local image paths because the schema JSON may not contain them. They must use session-relative paths so every other skill can resolve them by joining with `{session_root}`.

`assets_generation_queue_v{ASSETS_VERSION}.json` must map every manifest asset to its target file before image generation:

```json
{
  "session_id": "topic_no1_yyyymmdd",
  "version": "v001",
  "items": [
    {
      "asset_id": "asset_001",
      "asset_key": "char_001_turnaround",
      "asset_type": "character_turnaround",
      "priority": "required",
      "target_path": "assets/generated/v001/asset_001_char_001_turnaround.png",
      "prompt": "...",
      "negative_prompt": "...",
      "status": "queued"
    }
  ]
}
```

`assets_generation_status_v{ASSETS_VERSION}.json` must be updated after file verification and must be indexable by both `asset_id` and `asset_key`:

```json
{
  "session_id": "topic_no1_yyyymmdd",
  "version": "v001",
  "generated": [
    {
      "asset_id": "asset_001",
      "asset_key": "char_001_turnaround",
      "path": "assets/generated/v001/asset_001_char_001_turnaround.png",
      "size": [1024, 1536],
      "bytes": 123456,
      "qc_status": "pending_manual_qc"
    }
  ],
  "failed": [],
  "pending": []
}
```

If an image is not generated yet, leave the manifest asset `status` as `planned` and list it under `pending`; do not invent a file path as if it exists.

### 4.3 Generated full storyboard frame image paths

After reusable assets are planned or generated, generate the full storyboard image set under the same `assets/` stage:

```text
{session_root}/assets/storyboard_frames/{ASSETS_VERSION}/{shot_id}_A.png
{session_root}/assets/storyboard_frames/{ASSETS_VERSION}/{shot_id}_B.png
{session_root}/assets/storyboard_frames/{ASSETS_VERSION}/{shot_id}_C.png
```

Use named variants intentionally:

```text
A = hero keyframe / most representative shot beat
B = setup, before-state, insert, or asset-context frame
C = result, after-state, reaction, or continuity-completion frame
D = optional extra detail frame when the shot cannot read in one still
```

Required storyboard-frame sidecar files when frame generation is planned or run:

```text
{session_root}/assets/storyboard_frames/storyboard_frame_queue_v{ASSETS_VERSION}.json
{session_root}/assets/storyboard_frames/storyboard_frame_status_v{ASSETS_VERSION}.json
{session_root}/assets/storyboard_frames/storyboard_frames_manifest_v{ASSETS_VERSION}.json
{session_root}/assets/storyboard_frames/storyboard_frames_manifest_v{ASSETS_VERSION}.md
```

Optional convenience output:

```text
{session_root}/assets/storyboard_frames/contact_sheet_storyboard_frames_v{ASSETS_VERSION}.png
```

These storyboard-frame sidecars are operational outputs, not replacements for `assets_manifest_v{N}.json` and not governed by `assets-manifest.schema.json` unless a dedicated storyboard-frame schema is added later. They are the canonical place where downstream shot generators should look for complete still-frame references.

`storyboard_frame_queue_v{ASSETS_VERSION}.json` must list planned frames in shot order, variant order, and session-relative target paths:

```json
{
  "session_id": "topic_no1_yyyymmdd",
  "version": "v001",
  "source_storyboard_version": "v001",
  "source_assets_version": "v001",
  "frames": [
    {
      "shot_id": "shot_001",
      "variant": "A",
      "frame_id": "shot_001_A",
      "target_path": "assets/storyboard_frames/v001/shot_001_A.png",
      "asset_keys": ["char_001_turnaround", "scene_001_background"],
      "asset_image_paths": ["assets/generated/v001/asset_001_char_001_turnaround.png"],
      "prompt": "...",
      "negative_prompt": "...",
      "status": "queued"
    }
  ]
}
```

`storyboard_frame_status_v{ASSETS_VERSION}.json` must record generation/QC status with the same `frame_id`, `shot_id`, `variant`, and `path` fields:

```json
{
  "session_id": "topic_no1_yyyymmdd",
  "version": "v001",
  "frames": [
    {
      "frame_id": "shot_001_A",
      "shot_id": "shot_001",
      "variant": "A",
      "path": "assets/storyboard_frames/v001/shot_001_A.png",
      "size": [1024, 1536],
      "bytes": 123456,
      "status": "generated",
      "qc_status": "pending_manual_qc"
    }
  ],
  "missing_shot_ids": [],
  "failed": []
}
```

`storyboard_frames_manifest_v{ASSETS_VERSION}.json` is the stable downstream index. It must group frames by shot so a video shot generator can resolve reference stills without scraping Markdown:

```json
{
  "session_id": "topic_no1_yyyymmdd",
  "version": "v001",
  "source_storyboard_version": "v001",
  "source_assets_version": "v001",
  "shots": [
    {
      "shot_id": "shot_001",
      "frames": [
        {"variant": "A", "frame_id": "shot_001_A", "path": "assets/storyboard_frames/v001/shot_001_A.png", "status": "generated", "qc_status": "pending_manual_qc"}
      ]
    }
  ]
}
```

## 5. Asset Manifest Schema Rules

The JSON root must contain exactly the fields required by `assets-manifest.schema.json`:

```json
{
  "schema_version": "0.1.0",
  "session_id": "topic_no1_yyyymmdd",
  "version": "v001",
  "source_script_version": "v001",
  "source_storyboard_version": "v001",
  "title": "...",
  "asset_style_guide": {},
  "characters": [],
  "scenes": [],
  "props": [],
  "style_references": [],
  "shot_asset_map": [],
  "coverage": {},
  "generation_notes": {}
}
```

`generation_notes.skill` for new assets-stage manifests must be:

```json

# Doubao Seedance Shot Generator Skill

## 1. 使用场景

当 `storyboard` 和 `assets` 均已人工批准，且需要把每个分镜组织成豆包 Seedance 2.0 视频片段生成接口的调用参数时，使用本 Skill。

本 Skill 的职责是“组织接口参数”，不是直接承担创作审核，也不默认调用外部 API。

默认模式：

```text
request_planning
```

即生成每个 shot 的请求体、curl 模板和机器可读任务清单，等待人工审核或后续 submitter 执行。

## 2. 上游输入

必须读取：

```text
{session_root}/storyboard/storyboard_v{N}.json
{session_root}/assets/assets_manifest_v{N}.json
{session_root}/assets/generated/assets_generation_status_v{N}.json              # if asset images were generated
{session_root}/assets/storyboard_frames/storyboard_frames_manifest_v{N}.json    # if storyboard frames were generated
{session_root}/production_state.json
```

制片人 Agent 必须确认：

- storyboard status = approved
- assets status = approved
- storyboard/assets 均未 stale
- 当前使用的是 production_state.json 指向的版本

## 3. 输出产物

输出目录：

```text
{session_root}/shots/
```

必须生成：

```text
{session_root}/shots/shot_tasks_v001.md
{session_root}/shots/shot_tasks_v001.json
{session_root}/shots/review_v001.md
{session_root}/shots/review_v001.json
```

`shot_tasks_v{N}.json` 必须符合：

```text
video-agent/schemas/shot-generation-tasks.schema.json
```

review JSON 必须符合：

```text
video-agent/schemas/review.schema.json
```

## 4. 目标接口

接口 endpoint：

```text
POST https://ark.cn-beijing.volces.com/api/v3/contents/generations/tasks
```

默认模型：

```text
doubao-seedance-2-0-fast-260128
```

请求体结构：

```json
{
  "model": "doubao-seedance-2-0-fast-260128",
  "content": [
    {"type": "text", "text": "..."},
    {"type": "image_url", "image_url": {"url": "..."}, "role": "reference_image"},
    {"type": "video_url", "video_url": {"url": "..."}, "role": "reference_video"},
    {"type": "audio_url", "audio_url": {"url": "..."}, "role": "reference_audio"}
  ],
  "generate_audio": true,
  "ratio": "9:16",
  "duration": 6,
  "watermark": false
}
```

## 5. 鉴权规则

禁止把 token 写入产物文件。

所有 curl 模板统一使用环境变量占位：

```text
Authorization: Bearer ${ARK_API_KEY}
```

真实提交前由执行器从环境变量读取：

```text
ARK_API_KEY
```

如果没有 `ARK_API_KEY`，只能生成 request package，不得提交 API。

## 6. Prompt 组织规则

每个 shot 的 text prompt 应合并：

1. 全局风格：来自 assets.asset_style_guide。
2. 分镜信息：shot.scene、visual、character_action、dialogue_or_voiceover、sound、transition、notes。
3. 关联资产：shot_asset_map 中的 asset_keys 和资产描述。
4. 时长：使用 storyboard shot.duration_sec。
5. 比例：优先使用 storyboard.aspect_ratio；本项目竖屏使用 `9:16`。
6. 连续性约束：必须保留资产规划中的芯片拆分规则。
7. 负面约束：低质量、模糊、logo、水印、横屏构图、角色不一致等。

推荐 prompt 格式：

```text
竖屏 9:16，赛博朋克电影预告片风格。生成 shot_001，时长 5 秒。
画面：...
角色动作：...
旁白/对白：...
声音：...
转场：...
必须使用资产锚点：...
连续性：...
避免：...
```

## 7. reference media 规则

`assets_manifest_v{N}.json` 只描述资产和 shot-to-asset 关系，不包含本地图片路径。查找图片时必须按以下顺序读取 sidecar：

1. Reusable asset images: `assets/generated/assets_generation_status_v{N}.json.generated[]`，用 `asset_key` 或 `asset_id` 匹配，读取其中 session-relative `path`。
2. Full storyboard frame stills: `assets/storyboard_frames/storyboard_frames_manifest_v{N}.json.shots[]`，用 `shot_id` 匹配，读取 `frames[].path`。
3. 如果 sidecar 不存在或对应 path 不存在，只能退化为 text-only；不得从 Markdown 猜路径，不得编造 URL。

当前 Doubao Ark 接口的 `image_url` 需要可由服务端访问的 URL；本地 `assets/.../*.png` 路径不能直接填入 `image_url.url`，除非已经由后续上传/对象存储步骤转换为公开或 provider 可访问 URL。

- 如果某个资产已经有可公开访问的图片 URL，加入 `content`：
  `{"type":"image_url","image_url":{"url":"..."},"role":"reference_image"}`。
- 如果只有本地图片路径，把它记录在 Markdown/notes 作为待上传参考，不要写进 `request.body.content` 的 `image_url`。
- 如果有参考视频 URL，加入 `video_url`，role 为 `reference_video`。
- 如果有参考音频 URL，加入 `audio_url`，role 为 `reference_audio`。
- 没有 URL 时，必须仍生成 text-only 请求体，不能编造 URL。

## 8. 输出状态

生成 request package 后，制片人 Agent 应更新：

```json
{
  "versions": {"shots": "v001"},
  "stages": {
    "shots": {
      "status": "pending_human_review",
      "version": "v001",
      "artifacts": [
        "shots/shot_tasks_v001.md",
        "shots/shot_tasks_v001.json",
        "shots/review_v001.md",
        "shots/review_v001.json"
      ]
    }
  },
  "current_stage": "shots"
}
```

如果之前已经生成 `reports/production_package.md`，插入 shots 阶段后必须将 package 标记为 stale，因为 package 尚未包含视频片段生成任务。

## 9. 禁止行为

- 禁止在没有人工批准 assets 前生成 shots 请求。
- 禁止使用 rejected 或 stale 的 storyboard/assets。
- 禁止把真实 API token 写入 JSON、Markdown、curl 模板或日志。
- 禁止在用户只要求参数规划时直接 POST 外部 API。
- 禁止编造 reference image/video/audio URL。

## 10. 自动审核 gate

只有当：

```text
shot_tasks_v001.json schema valid
每个 storyboard shot 都有一个 task
每个 task.request.body.content 至少包含一个 text item
每个 task.duration 等于对应 shot.duration_sec
每个 task.ratio 等于项目目标比例
Authorization 使用 Bearer ${ARK_API_KEY} 占位而不是真 token
verdict = pass
blocking_issues = []
```

才允许请求人工审核。

## 11. 工具脚本

本 Skill 附带脚本：

```text
video-agent/skills/doubao-seedance-shot-generator/scripts/build_seedance_shot_tasks.py
```

用法：

```bash
python video-agent/skills/doubao-seedance-shot-generator/scripts/build_seedance_shot_tasks.py \
  --session-root video-agent/video-generation-session/cyber_cat_trailer/cyber_cat_trailer_no1_20260503
```

脚本只生成请求参数包和自动审核文件，不提交 API。

```

# Simple Video Editor Skill

## 使用场景

当用户希望先用已经生成的部分 shot 视频片段测试混剪/剪辑阶段，而不是等待全部 shots 生成完成时，使用本 Skill。

当前 MVP 目标：

- 读取 `{session_root}/shots/dreamina_submission_v{N}.json`
- 找到 `status == human_approved` 且存在 `local_video_path` 的 shot
- 按 order 排序
- 使用 ffmpeg 生成 rough cut
- 输出 edit manifest Markdown/JSON 和 review Markdown/JSON
- 允许 partial edit test：例如只用 shot_001 + shot_002 测试剪辑流程

## 输入

```text
{session_root}/production_state.json
{session_root}/shots/dreamina_submission_v001.json
{session_root}/shots/shot_generation_skip_v001.json   # 可选，用于 partial edit test
```

## 输出

```text
{session_root}/edit/edit_manifest_v001.md
{session_root}/edit/edit_manifest_v001.json
{session_root}/edit/rough_cut_v001.mp4
{session_root}/edit/concat_list_v001.txt
{session_root}/edit/ffmpeg_v001.log
{session_root}/edit/review_v001.md
{session_root}/edit/review_v001.json
```

## 规则

1. 不要求全部 9 个 shot 都已生成；如果用户明确要求测试 edit skill，可用 partial edit test。
2. 必须记录 `coverage.is_partial_edit_test = true` 和 skipped shot IDs。
3. 只使用存在且非空的视频文件。
4. 生成后必须用 ffprobe 验证 rough cut 存在、可读、有时长、有 9:16 分辨率。
5. partial edit test 通过不代表最终 production package 完成；后续完整视频仍需补齐剩余 shots 或明确接受短版。
6. 不修改源视频，只在 `edit/` 下生成新产物。

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""End-to-end contract test for the Video Agent planning chain.

This intentionally uses local/dry-run artifacts only: no external image/video API
is called. It verifies the canonical README chain can produce schema-valid files
through package_complete using session-local artifacts.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import json
import subprocess
from pathlib import Path

from jsonschema import Draft202012Validator

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
SESSION = ROOT / "video-generation-session/ruisha_demo_video_script/ruisha_demo_video_script_no1_20260507"
VERSION = "v001"


def load_json(path: Path):
    return json.loads(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))


def validate(schema_name: str, obj):
    schema = load_json(ROOT / "schemas" / schema_name)
    errors = sorted(Draft202012Validator(schema).iter_errors(obj), key=lambda e: list(e.path))
    assert not errors, "\n".join(f"{list(e.path)}: {e.message}" for e in errors[:20])


def run(cmd: list[str]):
    proc = subprocess.run(cmd, cwd=ROOT, text=True, capture_output=True)
    assert proc.returncode == 0, f"command failed: {' '.join(cmd)}\nSTDOUT:\n{proc.stdout}\nSTDERR:\n{proc.stderr}"
    return proc


def test_video_agent_complete_planning_chain_contract():
    # Existing planning chain adapter should pass through assets.
    run(["python3", "tools/run_ruisha_asset_planner_chain_test.py"])
    run(["python3", "tools/validate_ruisha_asset_planner_chain_test.py"])

    # Shots request package should be buildable after approved storyboard/assets.
    state = load_json(SESSION / "production_state.json")
    state["stages"]["assets"]["status"] = "approved"
    state["current_stage"] = "shots"
    state["human_approvals"].append({
        "timestamp": state["updated_at"],
        "stage": "assets",
        "version": VERSION,
        "decision": "approved",
        "comment": "full-chain contract test approves assets planning output",
    })
    (SESSION / "production_state.json").write_text(json.dumps(state, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2) + "\n", encoding="utf-8")
    validate("production-state.schema.json", state)

    run([
        "python3",
        "skills/doubao-seedance-shot-generator/scripts/build_seedance_shot_tasks.py",
        "--session-root",
        str(SESSION),
        "--version",
        VERSION,
    ])

    shot_tasks = load_json(SESSION / "shots" / f"shot_tasks_{VERSION}.json")
    validate("shot-generation-tasks.schema.json", shot_tasks)
    assert len(shot_tasks["tasks"]) == len(load_json(SESSION / "storyboard" / f"storyboard_{VERSION}.json")["shots"])
    assert shot_tasks["coverage"]["missing_shot_ids"] == []
    assert shot_tasks["coverage"]["unresolved_asset_keys"] == []

    # Submission stage should support dry-run without calling Dreamina.
    run([
        "python3",
        "skills/dreamina-shot-submitter/scripts/submit_next_dreamina_shot.py",
        "--session-root",
        str(SESSION),
        "--version",
        VERSION,
        "--dry-run",
    ])
    submission = load_json(SESSION / "shots" / f"dreamina_submission_{VERSION}.json")
    validate("dreamina-shot-submission.schema.json", submission)
    assert submission["submissions"][0]["status"] == "dry_run"

    # A full-chain planning test still needs local approved videos for edit.
    # The contract expects a helper to create deterministic placeholder clips and
    # package_complete artifacts after dry-run submission.
    run([
        "python3",
        "tools/run_full_chain_planning_test.py",
        "--session-root",
        str(SESSION),
        "--version",
        VERSION,
    ])

    edit_manifest = load_json(SESSION / "edit" / f"edit_manifest_{VERSION}.json")
    validate("edit-manifest.schema.json", edit_manifest)
    assert Path(edit_manifest["outputs"]["rough_cut_path"]).exists()
    assert edit_manifest["coverage"]["used_shot_ids"]

    final_state = load_json(SESSION / "production_state.json")
    validate("production-state.schema.json", final_state)
    assert final_state["current_stage"] == "package_complete"
    assert final_state["versions"]["shots"] == VERSION
    assert final_state["versions"]["edit"] == VERSION
    assert final_state["stages"]["package"]["status"] == "complete"
    assert (SESSION / "reports" / "production_package.md").exists()
    assert (SESSION / "reports" / f"full_chain_test_summary_{VERSION}.json").exists()
