#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Build Doubao Seedance shot generation request packages for a video-agent session.

This script does not submit API requests. It writes:
  shots/shot_tasks_v001.md
  shots/shot_tasks_v001.json
  shots/review_v001.md
  shots/review_v001.json
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import json
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any

try:
    from jsonschema import Draft202012Validator
except Exception as exc:  # pragma: no cover
    raise SystemExit("jsonschema is required to validate shot task output") from exc

ENDPOINT = "https://ark.cn-beijing.volces.com/api/v3/contents/generations/tasks"
MODEL = "doubao-seedance-2-0-fast-260128"
AUTH_ENV = "ARK_API_KEY"


def load_json(path: Path) -> dict[str, Any]:
    return json.loads(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))


def asset_index(assets: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, dict[str, Any]]:
    idx: dict[str, dict[str, Any]] = {}
    for section in ["characters", "scenes", "props", "style_references"]:
        for asset in assets.get(section, []):
            idx[asset["asset_key"]] = asset
    return idx


def shot_asset_map(assets: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, list[str]]:
    return {item["shot_id"]: item.get("asset_keys", []) for item in assets.get("shot_asset_map", [])}


def build_prompt(shot: dict[str, Any], asset_keys: list[str], assets_by_key: dict[str, dict[str, Any]], style: dict[str, Any]) -> str:
    asset_lines = []
    for key in asset_keys:
        asset = assets_by_key.get(key)
        if asset:
            asset_lines.append(f"- {key}: {asset.get('name')}；{asset.get('description')}")
        else:
            asset_lines.append(f"- {key}: 未在 assets manifest 中找到详细描述")
    sound = shot.get("sound", {})
    sound_text = "；".join([f"{k}: {v}" for k, v in sound.items()]) if isinstance(sound, dict) else str(sound)
    consistency = "第一场只允许出现破碎坐标/数据残影；中段可出现失踪芯片投影；实体记忆芯片只允许在 shot_008 塔心透明舱内出现。"
    return "\n".join([
        f"竖屏 {shot.get('aspect_ratio', style.get('aspect_ratio', '9:16'))}，{style.get('visual_style', '赛博朋克电影预告片风格')}。",
        f"生成 {shot['shot_id']}，时长 {shot['duration_sec']} 秒，镜头顺序 {shot['order']}。",
        f"镜头规格：{shot.get('shot_size')}，机位：{shot.get('camera_angle')}，运动：{shot.get('camera_movement')}。",
        f"场景：{shot.get('scene')}。",
        f"画面：{shot.get('visual')}。",
        f"角色动作：{shot.get('character_action')}。",
        f"旁白/对白：{shot.get('dialogue_or_voiceover')}。",
        f"声音设计：{sound_text}。",
        f"转场：{shot.get('transition')}。",
        "必须使用资产锚点：",
        *asset_lines,
        f"连续性约束：{consistency}",
        f"避免：{shot.get('negative_prompt') or style.get('negative_prompt', '')}",
        f"备注：{shot.get('notes', '')}",
    ])


def build_curl(body: dict[str, Any]) -> str:
    body_json = json.dumps(body, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2)
    return "\n".join([
        f"curl -X POST {ENDPOINT} \\",
        "  -H \"Content-Type: application/json\" \\",
        f"  -H \"Authorization: Bearer ${{{AUTH_ENV}}}\" \\",
        "  -d '" + body_json.replace("'", "'\\''") + "'",
    ])


def main() -> None:
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    parser.add_argument("--session-root", required=True)
    parser.add_argument("--version", default="v001")
    parser.add_argument("--model", default=MODEL)
    parser.add_argument("--ratio", default=None)
    parser.add_argument("--generate-audio", action="store_true", default=True)
    parser.add_argument("--no-generate-audio", dest="generate_audio", action="store_false")
    parser.add_argument("--watermark", action="store_true", default=False)
    args = parser.parse_args()

    session_root = Path(args.session_root).resolve()
    video_root = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[3]
    shots_dir = session_root / "shots"
    shots_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

    state = load_json(session_root / "production_state.json")
    storyboard_version = state["versions"]["storyboard"]
    assets_version = state["versions"]["assets"]
    storyboard = load_json(session_root / "storyboard" / f"storyboard_{storyboard_version}.json")
    assets = load_json(session_root / "assets" / f"assets_manifest_{assets_version}.json")

    if state["stages"]["storyboard"]["status"] != "approved":
        raise SystemExit("storyboard must be approved before building shot tasks")
    if state["stages"]["assets"]["status"] != "approved":
        raise SystemExit("assets must be approved before building shot tasks")
    if state["stages"]["storyboard"].get("stale_reason") or state["stages"]["assets"].get("stale_reason"):
        raise SystemExit("stale storyboard/assets cannot be used")

    style = assets["asset_style_guide"]
    ratio = args.ratio or storyboard.get("aspect_ratio") or style.get("aspect_ratio") or state["target"]["aspect_ratio"]
    assets_by_key = asset_index(assets)
    shot_to_assets = shot_asset_map(assets)
    now = datetime.now(timezone.utc).strftime("%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ")

    tasks = []
    missing = []
    unresolved_keys = []
    for idx, shot in enumerate(storyboard["shots"], start=1):
        shot_id = shot["shot_id"]
        asset_keys = shot_to_assets.get(shot_id, [])
        if not asset_keys:
            missing.append(shot_id)
        unresolved_keys.extend([k for k in asset_keys if k not in assets_by_key])
        prompt = build_prompt(shot, asset_keys, assets_by_key, style)
        body = {
            "model": args.model,
            "content": [{"type": "text", "text": prompt}],
            "generate_audio": bool(args.generate_audio),
            "ratio": ratio,
            "duration": int(shot["duration_sec"]),
            "watermark": bool(args.watermark),
        }
        tasks.append({
            "task_id": f"seedance_task_{idx:03d}",
            "shot_id": shot_id,
            "order": int(shot["order"]),
            "duration": int(shot["duration_sec"]),
            "status": "request_prepared",
            "asset_keys": asset_keys,
            "prompt_text": prompt,
            "request": {
                "method": "POST",
                "url": ENDPOINT,
                "headers": {"Content-Type": "application/json", "Authorization": f"Bearer ${{{AUTH_ENV}}}"},
                "body": body,
            },
            "curl_template": build_curl(body),
            "notes": "Request package only; not submitted to Ark API.",
        })

    package = {
        "schema_version": "0.1.0",
        "session_id": state["project_id"],
        "version": args.version,
        "source_storyboard_version": storyboard_version,
        "source_assets_version": assets_version,
        "provider": "volcengine_ark",
        "model": args.model,
        "endpoint": ENDPOINT,
        "generation_defaults": {
            "generate_audio": bool(args.generate_audio),
            "ratio": ratio,
            "watermark": bool(args.watermark),
            "auth_env_var": AUTH_ENV,
            "content_type": "application/json",
        },
        "tasks": tasks,
        "coverage": {
            "covered_shot_ids": [t["shot_id"] for t in tasks],
            "missing_shot_ids": missing,
            "task_count": len(tasks),
            "unresolved_asset_keys": sorted(set(unresolved_keys)),
        },
        "generation_notes": {
            "skill": "doubao-seedance-shot-generator",
            "mode": "request_planning",
            "created_at": now,
            "api_call_policy": "No external API call is made by this script; Authorization uses ARK_API_KEY placeholder.",
        },
    }

    schema = load_json(video_root / "schemas/shot-generation-tasks.schema.json")
    Draft202012Validator(schema).validate(package)

    json_path = shots_dir / f"shot_tasks_{args.version}.json"
    md_path = shots_dir / f"shot_tasks_{args.version}.md"
    json_path.write_text(json.dumps(package, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2) + "\n", encoding="utf-8")

    md_lines = [
        f"# Doubao Seedance Shot Tasks {args.version}", "",
        f"- Session ID: {package['session_id']}",
        f"- Model: {args.model}",
        f"- Endpoint: {ENDPOINT}",
        f"- Ratio: {ratio}",
        f"- Generate audio: {bool(args.generate_audio)}",
        f"- Watermark: {bool(args.watermark)}",
        f"- Auth: Bearer ${{{AUTH_ENV}}}",
        f"- Task count: {len(tasks)}", "",
        "## 注意", "", "本文件只包含请求参数和 curl 模板，尚未提交 API。不要把真实 token 写入本文件。", "",
        "## Tasks", "",
    ]
    for task in tasks:
        md_lines.extend([
            f"### {task['task_id']} / {task['shot_id']}", "",
            f"- Order: {task['order']}",
            f"- Duration: {task['duration']} 秒",
            f"- Asset keys: {', '.join(task['asset_keys'])}",
            "- Status: request_prepared", "",
            "Prompt:", "", "```text", task["prompt_text"], "```", "",
            "Curl template:", "", "```bash", task["curl_template"], "```", "",
        ])
    md_path.write_text("\n".join(md_lines), encoding="utf-8")

    blocking = []
    if missing:
        blocking.append({"id":"shots-blocking-missing","severity":"high","category":"coverage","message":"Some storyboard shots are missing tasks.","location":"shots/shot_tasks_v001.json","suggestion":"Regenerate tasks for all storyboard shots."})
    if unresolved_keys:
        blocking.append({"id":"shots-blocking-assets","severity":"high","category":"coverage","message":"Some task asset_keys are not found in assets manifest.","location":"shots/shot_tasks_v001.json","suggestion":"Fix shot_asset_map or assets manifest."})
    review = {
        "schema_version": "0.1.0",
        "session_id": state["project_id"],
        "stage": "shots",
        "version": args.version,
        "reviewer": {"name": "shot-task-reviewer", "mode": "automated"},
        "verdict": "pass" if not blocking else "needs_revision",
        "score": 0.92 if not blocking else 0.65,
        "blocking_issues": blocking,
        "non_blocking_issues": [{"id":"shots-nonblocking-001","severity":"low","category":"quality","message":"当前只生成 request package，尚未提交 Doubao Seedance API。","location":"shots/shot_tasks_v001.md","suggestion":"人工审核通过后再接入 submitter/轮询/下载。"}],
        "approved_items": ["shots/shot_tasks_v001.md", "shots/shot_tasks_v001.json"],
        "suggestions": [{"id":"shots-suggestion-001","message":"真实资产图片 URL 生成后，可把角色三视图和关键场景图加入 content 的 reference_image。","priority":"medium"}],
        "checked_artifacts": [
            {"path":"storyboard/storyboard_v001.json","type":"json","exists":True,"parse_status":"passed"},
            {"path":"assets/assets_manifest_v001.json","type":"json","exists":True,"parse_status":"passed"},
            {"path":"shots/shot_tasks_v001.md","type":"markdown","exists":True,"parse_status":"not_applicable"},
            {"path":"shots/shot_tasks_v001.json","type":"json","exists":True,"parse_status":"passed"},
        ],
        "checklist": [
            {"id":"shots-check-001","description":"shot task json validates against schema.","status":"passed","details":"Validated with Draft202012Validator."},
            {"id":"shots-check-002","description":"all storyboard shots have tasks.","status":"passed" if not missing else "failed","details":f"covered={len(tasks)}/{len(storyboard['shots'])}; missing={missing}"},
            {"id":"shots-check-003","description":"each task contains text content and ARK_API_KEY placeholder.","status":"passed","details":"No real token stored."},
            {"id":"shots-check-004","description":"no external API call submitted.","status":"passed","details":"request_planning mode only."},
        ],
        "created_at": now,
    }
    review_schema = load_json(video_root / "schemas/review.schema.json")
    Draft202012Validator(review_schema).validate(review)
    (shots_dir / f"review_{args.version}.json").write_text(json.dumps(review, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2) + "\n", encoding="utf-8")
    review_md = f"""# Shots 自动审核报告 {args.version}\n\n- 会话 ID：{state['project_id']}\n- 阶段：shots\n- 版本：{args.version}\n- 审核器：shot-task-reviewer\n- 结论：{review['verdict']}\n- 分数：{review['score']}\n- Task count：{len(tasks)}\n- Covered shots：{len(tasks)}/{len(storyboard['shots'])}\n- Missing shots：{', '.join(missing) or 'none'}\n- Unresolved asset keys：{', '.join(sorted(set(unresolved_keys))) or 'none'}\n- Endpoint：{ENDPOINT}\n- Model：{args.model}\n- Ratio：{ratio}\n- Generate audio：{bool(args.generate_audio)}\n- Watermark：{bool(args.watermark)}\n\n## Blocking issues\n\n{('无。' if not blocking else chr(10).join('- '+b['message'] for b in blocking))}\n\n## Non-blocking issues\n\n- 当前只生成 request package，尚未提交 Doubao Seedance API。\n\n## 人工审核建议\n\n如果请求参数组织方式正确，可回复：\n\n```text\n批准 shots，提交/进入视频生成任务阶段。\n```\n\n如果需要调整 prompt 或参数，可回复：\n\n```text\n修改：<你的修改意见>\n```\n"""
    (shots_dir / f"review_{args.version}.md").write_text(review_md, encoding="utf-8")

    print(json.dumps({"shot_tasks": str(json_path), "review": str(shots_dir / f"review_{args.version}.json"), "task_count": len(tasks), "verdict": review["verdict"]}, ensure_ascii=False))

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()


```

# Dreamina Shot Submitter Skill

## 1. 使用场景

当 `shots/shot_tasks_v{N}.json` 已经通过自动审核并获得人工批准后，使用即梦 `dreamina` CLI 提交真实视频生成任务。

本 Skill 采用严格串行策略：

```text
one_shot_at_a_time
```

即每次只提交一个 shot。当前 shot 生成完成并由用户确认视频效果后，才允许继续提交下一个 shot。

## 2. CLI 安装与自检

安装命令：

```bash
curl -fsSL https://jimeng.jianying.com/cli | bash
```

查看帮助：

```bash
dreamina -h
```

登录：

```bash
dreamina login
```

登录 debug：

```bash
dreamina login --debug
```

登录自检：

```bash
dreamina user_credit
```

只有 `dreamina user_credit` 能正常返回余额 JSON 时，才允许提交生成任务。

## 3. 本地文件与日志

即梦 CLI 会维护：

```text
~/.dreamina_cli/config.toml
~/.dreamina_cli/credential.json
~/.dreamina_cli/tasks.db
~/.dreamina_cli/logs/
```

排查问题时必须保留并检查 `~/.dreamina_cli/logs/`。

## 4. 必须读取

```text
{session_root}/production_state.json
{session_root}/shots/shot_tasks_v{N}.json
{session_root}/shots/review_v{N}.json
{session_root}/shots/dreamina_submission_v{N}.json  # 如果已存在，用于恢复队列状态
```

## 5. 前置条件

制片人 Agent 必须确认：

- `dreamina` 命令存在。
- `dreamina user_credit` 成功。
- `shots/review_v{N}.json.verdict == "pass"`。
- `shots/review_v{N}.json.blocking_issues == []`。
- 用户已经批准 shots request package。
- 每次真实提交前，制片人 Agent 必须向用户确认本次使用的 `model_version`；默认推荐 `seedance2.0fast_vip`，但不得在未确认模型时提交。
- 当前没有未完成、未下载或未人工确认的前序 shot。

## 5.1 模型与 Generator Command 选择

当前 Dreamina CLI 可用模型：

```text
seedance2.0
seedance2.0fast
seedance2.0_vip
seedance2.0fast_vip
```

默认模型：

```text
seedance2.0fast_vip
```

每次真实提交任务前，必须先向用户确认模型，例如：

```text
准备提交 shot_002。默认使用 model_version=seedance2.0fast_vip，Generator Command 自动选择为 text2video。是否确认？也可以指定 seedance2.0 / seedance2.0fast / seedance2.0_vip / seedance2.0fast_vip。
```

Generator Commands 由本次生成任务需要自动选择，不固定写死。当前支持：

```text
frames2video
image2image
image2video
image_upscale
multiframe2video
multimodal2video
text2image
text2video
```

自动选择规则：

- 纯文本生成视频：`text2video`
- 单张参考图动起来：`image2video`
- 多张图片组成连贯故事：`multiframe2video`
- 需要图片、视频、音频等全能参考，或需要 Seedance 2.0 全能参考模式：`multimodal2video`
- 首尾帧控制：`frames2video`
- 图片生成/改图/放大任务分别使用 `text2image`、`image2image`、`image_upscale`

每次提交前都要先运行对应子命令 help，例如：

```bash
dreamina text2video -h
dreamina multimodal2video -h
```

以实际 CLI 支持的参数为准。

## 6. 提交流程

1. 从 `shot_tasks_v{N}.json.tasks[]` 中找到 order 最小且尚未人工批准的 shot。
2. 根据本次任务需要自动选择 Generator Command；当前纯文本 shot 默认使用 `text2video`，有全能参考/多模态参考时使用 `multimodal2video`。
3. 将该 shot 的 `prompt_text` 作为 Dreamina 生成命令的 `--prompt` 入参。
4. 根据 shot 参数设置：
   - `--duration=<duration>`
   - `--ratio=<ratio>`
   - `--video_resolution=720p`，默认先用 720p 降低成本和等待时间。
   - `--model_version=<confirmed_model_version>`，默认推荐 `seedance2.0fast_vip`，但每次提交前必须由用户确认。
   - `--poll=30`，默认等待 30 秒。
5. 只提交这一个 shot。
6. 保存命令 stdout/stderr、Generator Command、model_version 和解析结果。
7. 若轮询完成并返回视频 URL 或下载路径，进入该 shot 的人工效果确认门。
8. 不自动提交下一个 shot。

## 7. 推荐命令模板

文生视频：

```bash
dreamina text2video \
  --prompt="<shot prompt>" \
  --duration=<duration> \
  --ratio=9:16 \
  --video_resolution=720p \
  --model_version=seedance2.0fast_vip \
  --poll=30
```

脚本提交模板：

```bash
python video-agent/skills/dreamina-shot-submitter/scripts/submit_next_dreamina_shot.py \
  --session-root video-agent/video-generation-session/<session_id> \
  --generator-command=auto \
  --model-version=seedance2.0fast_vip \
  --model-confirmed
```

注意：`--model-confirmed` 只能在用户确认本次模型后使用。

查询结果：

```bash
dreamina query_result --submit_id=<submit_id>
```

查询并下载：

```bash
dreamina query_result --submit_id=<submit_id> --download_dir=/Users/edy/vedio-agent/hermes-agent-main/video-agent/video-generation-session/<session_id>/shots/downloads/shot_001
```

## 8. 输出契约

提交状态 JSON：

```text
{session_root}/shots/dreamina_submission_v001.json
```

人类可读摘要：

```text
{session_root}/shots/dreamina_submission_v001.md
```

CLI 原始响应：

```text
{session_root}/shots/dreamina_responses/{shot_id}_submit_response_v001.json
```

下载目录：

```text
{session_root}/shots/downloads/{shot_id}/
```

## 9. 人工审核门

每个 shot 生成完成后，制片人 Agent 必须停下，向用户请求效果确认：

```text
shot_001 已生成完成。

产物：
- response: ...
- video url/local path: ...

请审核效果：
1. 回复“批准 shot_001，继续 shot_002。”
2. 回复“修改 shot_001：...”让我返工当前 shot。
3. 回复“暂停”停止继续提交。
```

## 10. 禁止行为

- 禁止一次提交多个 shot。
- 禁止当前 shot 未人工确认前提交下一个 shot。
- 禁止未向用户确认本次 `model_version` 就提交真实任务。
- 禁止忽略 `dreamina user_credit` 失败继续提交。
- 禁止在 CLI 报错时跳过当前 shot。
- 禁止使用 stale 的 storyboard/assets/shot_tasks。

# Doubao Seedance Shot Submitter Skill

## 1. 使用场景

当 `shots/shot_tasks_v{N}.json` 已经通过自动审核并获得人工批准后，使用本 Skill 提交真实视频生成任务。

本 Skill 的核心策略是：

```text
one_shot_at_a_time
```

也就是一次只提交一个 shot。当前 shot 完成生成、下载/记录结果，并经过人工效果确认后，制片人 Agent 才能继续提交下一个 shot。

## 2. 必须读取

```text
{session_root}/production_state.json
{session_root}/shots/shot_tasks_v{N}.json
{session_root}/shots/review_v{N}.json
{session_root}/shots/submission_v{N}.json  # 如果已存在，用于恢复队列状态
```

## 3. 前置条件

制片人 Agent 必须确认：

- `shots/review_v{N}.json.verdict == "pass"`
- `shots/review_v{N}.json.blocking_issues == []`
- 用户已经批准 shots request package
- `shots/submission_v{N}.json` 中没有未完成且未审核的前序 shot
- 环境变量 `ARK_API_KEY` 已设置

## 4. 提交流程

1. 找到下一个可提交的 shot：
   - 优先选择 `shot_tasks_v{N}.json.tasks[]` 中 order 最小且尚未提交/批准的 shot。
2. 只提交这一个 shot。
3. 写入 provider 原始响应：

```text
{session_root}/shots/provider_responses/{shot_id}_submit_response_v{N}.json
```

4. 更新：

```text
{session_root}/shots/submission_v{N}.json
{session_root}/shots/submission_v{N}.md
{session_root}/production_state.json
{session_root}/reports/production_log.md
```

5. 停止，不自动提交下一个 shot。

## 5. 输出契约

提交阶段状态文件：

```text
{session_root}/shots/submission_v001.json
```

必须符合：

```text
video-agent/schemas/shot-submission.schema.json
```

Markdown 摘要：

```text
{session_root}/shots/submission_v001.md
```

Provider 响应：

```text
{session_root}/shots/provider_responses/{shot_id}_submit_response_v001.json
```

## 6. 鉴权规则

禁止把真实 token 写入任何产物文件。

真实提交时只能从环境变量读取：

```text
ARK_API_KEY
```

写入 JSON/Markdown 时只能记录：

```text
ARK_API_KEY is set / not set
```

不能记录 token 内容。

## 7. 人工审核门

每个 shot 生成完成后必须停下，请用户确认效果。

制片人 Agent 的提示格式：

```text
shot_001 已提交/生成完成。

产物：
- provider response: ...
- video url/local path: ...

请审核效果：
1. 回复“批准 shot_001，继续 shot_002。”
2. 回复“修改 shot_001：...”让我返工当前 shot。
3. 回复“暂停”停止继续提交。
```

只有用户批准当前 shot 后，才能提交下一个 shot。

## 8. 禁止行为

- 禁止一次性提交多个 shot。
- 禁止在当前 shot 未获人工效果确认前提交下一个 shot。
- 禁止把真实 `ARK_API_KEY` 写入文件或回复。
- 禁止使用 stale 的 storyboard/assets/shot_tasks。
- 禁止在 API 失败后跳过当前 shot 继续后续 shot。

Output:
## 如何在 Hermes 中使用视频 Agent

当前视频 Agent 采用“项目内 Skill + 文件化工作流”的方式维护，核心入口是：

```text
video-agent/skills/video-producer/SKILL.md
```

制片人 Skill 会负责：

1. 创建或恢复视频生成会话。
2. 维护每个会话的 `production_state.json`。
3. 调度 mock-scriptwriter、mock-storyboard、asset-planner 等子 Skill。
4. 调用 reviewers 规则进行自动审核。
5. 在每个关键阶段停下来等待人工审核。
6. 将所有阶段产物保存到当前 session 目录中。
7. 在上游产物变更后标记下游产物为 stale，避免继续使用过期结果。

### 推荐启动方式：在新 Hermes 会话中显式读取本地 Skill

进入 Hermes 项目根目录：

```bash
cd /Users/edy/vedio-agent/hermes-agent-main
hermes
```

然后在新的 Hermes 会话里输入类似下面的提示词：

```text
请作为视频生成 Agent 的制片人主控来工作。

请先读取并遵守这些文件：

1. video-agent/README.md
2. video-agent/skills/video-producer/SKILL.md
3. video-agent/skills/mock-scriptwriter/SKILL.md
4. video-agent/skills/mock-storyboard/SKILL.md
5. video-agent/skills/asset-planner/SKILL.md
6. video-agent/skills/reviewers/script-reviewer.md
7. video-agent/skills/reviewers/storyboard-reviewer.md
8. video-agent/skills/reviewers/asset-reviewer.md
9. video-agent/schemas/*.schema.json

我要创建一个新的视频生成会话。

计划名称：<你的计划名称>

需求：
<你的视频需求>

请按照 video-producer 的流程执行：
创建 session 目录，生成 production_state.json，先完成 brief 阶段，自动审核 brief，然后停下来等我人工批准。
```

示例：

```text
请作为视频生成 Agent 的制片人主控来工作。

请先读取并遵守这些文件：

1. video-agent/README.md
2. video-agent/skills/video-producer/SKILL.md
3. video-agent/skills/mock-scriptwriter/SKILL.md
4. video-agent/skills/mock-storyboard/SKILL.md
5. video-agent/skills/asset-planner/SKILL.md
6. video-agent/skills/reviewers/script-reviewer.md
7. video-agent/skills/reviewers/storyboard-reviewer.md
8. video-agent/skills/reviewers/asset-reviewer.md
9. video-agent/schemas/*.schema.json

我要创建一个新的视频生成 demo 会话。

计划名称：cyber_cat_trailer

需求：
做一个 60 秒竖屏短视频，主题是赛博朋克猫侦探，风格像电影预告片，中文旁白，目标平台抖音。

请按照 video-producer 的流程执行：
创建 session 目录，生成 production_state.json，先完成 brief 阶段，自动审核 brief，然后停下来等我人工批准。
不要直接进入 script 阶段。
```

这种方式最适合当前 MVP 阶段，因为所有 Skill、Schema、模板和 reviewer 规范都还维护在项目内的 `video-agent/` 目录下。

### 可选方式：注册为 Hermes 全局 Skill 后使用 `/skill`

如果希望在任意新的 Hermes 会话里直接使用 `/skill video-producer`，可以把项目内 Skill 软链接到 Hermes 全局 skills 目录：

```bash
mkdir -p ~/.hermes/skills

ln -sfn /Users/edy/vedio-agent/hermes-agent-main/video-agent/skills/video-producer ~/.hermes/skills/video-producer
ln -sfn /Users/edy/vedio-agent/hermes-agent-main/video-agent/skills/mock-scriptwriter ~/.hermes/skills/mock-scriptwriter
ln -sfn /Users/edy/vedio-agent/hermes-agent-main/video-agent/skills/mock-storyboard ~/.hermes/skills/mock-storyboard
ln -sfn /Users/edy/vedio-agent/hermes-agent-main/video-agent/skills/asset-planner ~/.hermes/skills/asset-planner
```

然后启动 Hermes：

```bash
cd /Users/edy/vedio-agent/hermes-agent-main
hermes
```

在会话中加载 Skill：

```text
/skill video-producer
/skill mock-scriptwriter
/skill mock-storyboard
/skill asset-planner
```

然后输入：

```text
创建一个新的视频生成会话。

计划名称：cyber_cat_trailer

需求：
做一个 60 秒竖屏短视频，主题是赛博朋克猫侦探，风格像电影预告片，中文旁白，目标平台抖音。

请先创建 session，然后完成 brief 阶段和 brief 自动审核，完成后停下来等我人工批准。
```

也可以在启动时预加载：

```bash
cd /Users/edy/vedio-agent/hermes-agent-main
hermes -s video-producer -s mock-scriptwriter -s mock-storyboard -s asset-planner
```

注意：当前 reviewers 是 `video-agent/skills/reviewers/*.md` 文档，不是独立 Hermes Skill 目录。制片人 Agent 应按需读取这些 reviewer 文档。

### 创建新视频生成会话

新建会话时必须提供“主题名称”。如果是同一主题的第一段剧本，默认创建 `no1`；如果是后续剧情，默认在同一主题目录下创建下一个编号，例如 `no2`。

主题名称会被规范化为 `topic_name`，并和 episode number、当前日期组合成 `session_id`：

```text
{topic_name}_no{episode_number}_{yyyymmdd}
```

例如：

```text
主题名称：cyber_cat_trailer
剧本编号：no1
日期：20260503
session_id：cyber_cat_trailer_no1_20260503
会话目录：video-agent/video-generation-session/cyber_cat_trailer/cyber_cat_trailer_no1_20260503/
```

续写时：

```text
主题名称：cyber_cat_trailer
剧本编号：no2
日期：20260506
session_id：cyber_cat_trailer_no2_20260506
会话目录：video-agent/video-generation-session/cyber_cat_trailer/cyber_cat_trailer_no2_20260506/
```

所有本次生成的文件都必须保存在该 session 目录下。

### 恢复已有视频生成会话

如果会话已经存在，可以在新的 Hermes 会话中说：

```text
请作为视频生成 Agent 制片人主控工作。
请读取 video-agent/README.md 和 video-agent/skills/video-producer/SKILL.md。

请恢复这个视频生成会话：

cyber_cat_trailer_no1_20260503

会话目录是：

video-agent/video-generation-session/cyber_cat_trailer/cyber_cat_trailer_no1_20260503/

请先读取 production_state.json，总结当前阶段、已有产物、哪些阶段已批准、是否存在 stale 产物，然后告诉我下一步应该做什么。
```

如果已经注册为全局 Skill，可以简化为：

```text
/skill video-producer

继续 cyber_cat_trailer_no1_20260503
```

### 从同一主题的上一段剧本继续创作后续剧情

如果要基于某个已完成或已批准的 session 继续写后续剧情，推荐不要覆盖原 session，而是在同一个 `topic_name` 目录下创建下一段剧本 session。

推荐目录：

```text
video-agent/video-generation-session/{topic_name}/{topic_name}_no{next_episode_number}_{yyyymmdd}/
```

例如，第一段和第二段应组织为：

```text
video-agent/video-generation-session/cyber_cat_trailer/cyber_cat_trailer_no1_20260503/
video-agent/video-generation-session/cyber_cat_trailer/cyber_cat_trailer_no2_20260506/
```

这比 `cyber_cat_trailer_continuation_20260506` 更容易区分：

- `cyber_cat_trailer/` 是主题/系列目录；
- `cyber_cat_trailer_no1_20260503/` 是第一段剧本；
- `cyber_cat_trailer_no2_20260506/` 是承接第一段的第二段剧本；
- 后续可以继续创建 `no3`、`no4`。

如果上一段仍是 legacy 扁平目录，例如：

```text
video-agent/video-generation-session/cyber_cat_trailer/cyber_cat_trailer_no1_20260503/
```

仍然可以作为 continuity source 读取；但新续写 session 默认创建到两层目录。

续写 session 应引用上一部的这些产物作为 continuity context：

```text
上一 session/production_state.json
上一 session/brief/normalized_brief.json
上一 session/script/script_v{N}.json
上一 session/script/script_v{N}.md
上一 session/storyboard/storyboard_v{N}.json
上一 session/storyboard/storyboard_v{N}.md
上一 session/assets/assets_manifest_v{N}.json
上一 session/reports/production_package.md
```

如果只想从“最后一个分镜”继续，至少要读取：

```text
上一 session/script/script_v{N}.json
上一 session/storyboard/storyboard_v{N}.json
```

并提取：

- 最后一场 scene 的剧情状态；
- 最后一个 shot 的画面、旁白、悬念钩子；
- 仍未解决的谜题、人物关系、道具状态；
- 不能打破的连续性约束；
- 已批准的角色和世界观设定。

以当前 demo 为例，续写起点是：

```text
上一 session：cyber_cat_trailer_no1_20260503
有效剧本：script/script_v002.json
有效分镜：storyboard/storyboard_v001.json
最后分镜：shot_009
续写钩子：墨爪机械义眼里浮现下一条坐标 NEON-09，画面切黑。
关键约束：第一部中芯片不是在第一场交给墨爪，而是先通过破碎坐标引出追查；续作必须延续“NEON-09 坐标”和城市记忆被唤醒后的后果。
```

可以在新的 Hermes 会话中这样说：

```text
请作为视频生成 Agent 制片人主控工作。
请读取 video-agent/README.md 和 video-agent/skills/video-producer/SKILL.md。

我要创建一个剧情延续的新视频生成 session。
主题名称：cyber_cat_trailer
剧本编号：no2

请参考上一段剧本 session：
video-agent/video-generation-session/cyber_cat_trailer/cyber_cat_trailer_no1_20260503/

请创建新的 session 目录：
video-agent/video-generation-session/cyber_cat_trailer/cyber_cat_trailer_no2_20260506/

请读取上一部的：
1. production_state.json
2. script/script_v002.json
3. script/script_v002.md
4. storyboard/storyboard_v001.json
5. storyboard/storyboard_v001.md
6. reports/production_package.md

续写要求：
从上一部最后一个分镜 shot_009 继续：墨爪机械义眼里出现 NEON-09 坐标，画面切黑。
请先生成 continuation brief，明确承接上一部的剧情状态、未解谜题、角色状态和连续性约束。
完成 brief 自动审核后停下来等我人工批准，不要直接进入 script。
```

续写流程仍然走同样的审核门：

```text
continuation brief -> script -> storyboard -> assets -> shots -> edit -> package
```

注意：续写不是简单把上一部文件复制一份。它应该创建新的 session，但在 `brief/normalized_brief.json` 或后续专门的 continuity artifact 中记录 `source_session_id`、`source_script_version`、`source_storyboard_version`、`continuation_starting_point` 和 `continuity_constraints`。

### 推荐的分阶段执行节奏

接入真实视频片段生成和混剪后，不要一次直接跑完整链路。推荐按阶段推进：

1. 创建 session + brief。
2. brief 自动审核通过后，等待人工批准。
3. 人工批准 brief 后，进入 script。
4. script 自动审核通过后，等待人工批准。
5. 人工批准 script 后，进入 storyboard。
6. storyboard 自动审核通过后，等待人工批准。
7. 人工批准 storyboard 后，进入 assets。
8. assets 自动审核通过后，等待人工批准。
9. 人工批准 assets 后，进入 shots request package：生成 `shots/shot_tasks_v{N}.md/json` 和 `shots/review_v{N}.*`，但不提交真实视频生成。
10. 人工批准 shots request package 后，进入 shots submission：使用 Dreamina CLI 或其他 submitter 逐个 shot 生成；每个 shot 生成后都要等人工效果确认。
11. 有足够可用 clips 后，进入 edit：使用 `simple-video-editor` 生成 rough cut / final cut 和 edit manifest。
12. edit 自动审核通过后，等待人工批准。
13. 人工批准 edit 后，进入 package：生成 `reports/production_package.md` 和版本化 package。
14. 最后运行完整验证，确认 `production_state.json.current_stage == package_complete`。

批准示例：

```text
批准 brief，进入 script 阶段。
```

```text
批准 script，进入 storyboard 阶段。
```

```text
批准 storyboard，进入 assets 阶段。
```

```text
批准 assets，生成 production package。
```

### 如何验证第一个 demo 跑通

以 `cyber_cat_trailer_no1_20260503` 为例，完整跑通后，至少应该存在：

```text
video-agent/video-generation-session/cyber_cat_trailer/cyber_cat_trailer_no1_20260503/
  production_state.json

  brief/
    user_brief.md
    normalized_brief.json
    review_v001.md
    review_v001.json

  script/
    script_v001.md
    script_v001.json
    review_v001.md
    review_v001.json

  storyboard/
    storyboard_v001.md
    storyboard_v001.json
    storyboard_v001.csv
    review_v001.md
    review_v001.json

  assets/
    assets_manifest_v001.md
    assets_manifest_v001.json
    generated/v001/
    generated/assets_generation_queue_v001.json
    generated/assets_generation_status_v001.json
    storyboard_frames/v001/
    storyboard_frames/storyboard_frame_queue_v001.json
    storyboard_frames/storyboard_frame_status_v001.json
    storyboard_frames/storyboard_frames_manifest_v001.json
    storyboard_frames/storyboard_frames_manifest_v001.md
    review_v001.md
    review_v001.json

  reviews/
    human_review_log.md

  reports/
    production_log.md
    production_package.md
```

最低验收标准：

1. 文件齐全，且非空。
2. 所有 JSON 都能正常 parse。
3. 核心 JSON 通过对应 schema 校验。
4. 每个阶段的 `review_v001.json` 都满足：

```text
verdict = pass
blocking_issues = []

```

> TOOL

tool_result
id: call_XU7s7OqHdcGtMizdCHhGbwh5
```
Chunk ID: 2952ee
Wall time: 0.0000 seconds
Process exited with code 0
Original token count: 6445
Output:
3. After installation, require login/self-check before generation:

```bash
dreamina login
dreamina user_credit
```

Use `dreamina login --debug` for login hangs. If `dreamina user_credit` fails, stop and do not submit generation tasks.
4. Persist Dreamina-specific workflow docs/artifacts project-locally when adding this backend:

```text
video-agent/skills/dreamina-shot-submitter/SKILL.md
video-agent/skills/dreamina-shot-submitter/scripts/submit_next_dreamina_shot.py
video-agent/schemas/dreamina-shot-submission.schema.json
shots/dreamina_submission_v{N}.md
shots/dreamina_submission_v{N}.json
shots/dreamina_responses/{shot_id}_submit_response_v{N}.json
shots/downloads/{shot_id}/
```

5. Before real submission, inspect `dreamina text2video -h` and make the project-local submitter match the actual CLI flags/support. Dreamina currently documents `--video_resolution=720p` with lowercase `p`; using stale examples such as `720P` may fail. Patch the project-local skill/script before submitting if help output and docs differ.
6. Choose the Dreamina Generator Command automatically for the task instead of hardcoding `text2video`:

- pure text-to-video shots → `text2video`
- one reference image animated into video → `image2video`
- multiple images forming a coherent story → `multiframe2video`
- all-around/multimodal references or Seedance 2.0 reference workflows → `multimodal2video`
- first/last frame control → `frames2video`
- image generation/edit/upscale tasks → `text2image`, `image2image`, or `image_upscale`

Before real submission, ask the user to confirm the `model_version`. The default recommendation is `seedance2.0fast_vip`, but the user may choose `seedance2.0`, `seedance2.0fast`, `seedance2.0_vip`, or `seedance2.0fast_vip`. Do not submit with `--model-confirmed` unless the user has confirmed the model in the current shot submission flow.

7. Invoke the selected Dreamina command with the current shot prompt and confirmed model, usually:

```bash
dreamina text2video \
  --prompt="<shot prompt>" \
  --duration=<duration> \
  --ratio=9:16 \
  --video_resolution=720p \
  --model_version=seedance2.0fast_vip \
  --poll=30
```

For the project-local submitter, use:

```bash
python video-agent/skills/dreamina-shot-submitter/scripts/submit_next_dreamina_shot.py \
  --session-root video-agent/video-generation-session/<topic_name>/<session_id> \
  --generator-command=auto \
  --model-version=<confirmed_model_version> \
  --model-confirmed
```

8. Enforce the same `one_shot_at_a_time` gate: submit only the lowest-order shot not yet human-approved; block all later shots until the current shot has a generated result and the user approves the visual effect. A previous `dry_run` record for the same shot is not human approval and should not advance the queue; the real submission should still target that shot.
9. If the CLI returns a `submit_id` with `gen_status=querying`, treat the submit as successful but incomplete. Store the `submit_id`, queue info, and submit response. For follow-up download queries, prefer an absolute `--download_dir` resolved from the live session root; relative paths can download into the wrong location if the working directory changes.

```bash
dreamina query_result --submit_id=<submit_id>
dreamina query_result --submit_id=<submit_id> --download_dir=/absolute/path/to/video-agent/video-generation-session/<topic_name>/<session_id>/shots/downloads/<shot_id>
```

After a successful query, verify the local media file exists and has nonzero size before moving the shot to `pending_human_effect_review`.

10. Store each query response separately, e.g. `shots/dreamina_responses/{shot_id}_query_response_v{N}.json`, and update the human-readable submission Markdown with latest `gen_status`, queue status/index, absolute requested download directory, and local media path. Do not ask for effect approval until `gen_status=success` and a video URL or verified local media path exists.
11. Store stdout/stderr and parsed submit/video handles in the Dreamina response JSON. Never skip a failed or still-querying current shot to continue with later shots.
12. If troubleshooting is needed, mention the command run, the error description, and logs under `~/.dreamina_cli/logs/`.

## Partial edit / rough cut test checklist

When the user says they want to skip or postpone remaining shot generation and use already generated clips to test the edit/mixdown skill, treat this as a partial edit test under the `edit` stage, not as final package completion:

1. Re-read `production_state.json`, `shots/dreamina_submission_v{N}.json` or the active provider submission file, the edit skill doc, `edit-manifest.schema.json`, and `review.schema.json`.
2. Before editing, resolve any currently submitted shot if possible: query/download the current provider result with an absolute download directory, then update the submission JSON/Markdown so generated clips are not missed.
3. Record the user decision to skip/postpone the remaining shot IDs for this edit test in session artifacts such as:

```text
shots/shot_generation_skip_v{N}.md
shots/shot_generation_skip_v{N}.json
```

The skip record should include `skipped_for_current_edit_test`, the reason, and whether the decision is temporary.
4. Only use local video files that are verified to exist and have nonzero size. Prefer clips with `status == human_approved`; if the user explicitly says to use newly generated clips for testing, record that decision before including them.
5. Generate edit artifacts under:

```text
edit/edit_manifest_v{N}.md
edit/edit_manifest_v{N}.json
edit/rough_cut_v{N}.mp4
edit/concat_list_v{N}.txt
edit/ffmpeg_v{N}.log
edit/review_v{N}.md
edit/review_v{N}.json
```

6. The edit manifest must clearly mark partial coverage, for example `coverage.is_partial_edit_test = true`, `used_shot_ids`, `skipped_shot_ids`, and the reason. This prevents a two-clip rough cut from being mistaken for the final 60-second video.
7. Use `ffprobe` to verify the rough cut after `ffmpeg` runs. At minimum record duration, width, height, FPS, codec/format, and file size.
8. Validate `edit_manifest_v{N}.json` against `edit-manifest.schema.json`, `edit/review_v{N}.json` against `review.schema.json`, and `production_state.json` against `production-state.schema.json`.
9. Update `production_state.json.current_stage = "edit"`, set `stages.edit.status = "pending_human_review"`, and keep `stages.package.status = "stale"` or `not_started` unless the user explicitly accepts the partial rough cut as the final package.
10. Stop at the edit human-review gate. Ask the user to approve the rough cut, request edit changes, resume remaining shot generation, or pause.
11. If a schema validation error appears after updating state or review files, fix the artifact shape and the generating script before reporting success, then re-run validation.

## Session directory migration checklist

When the user asks to convert an existing legacy video session directory into the newer topic/session layout, treat it as a migration task, not a creative regeneration task.

Class of task: move an already-produced video-agent session to a new session root while preserving all artifacts, internal references, state validity, and media usability.

1. Re-read `video-agent/README.md`, `video-agent/skills/video-producer/SKILL.md`, and the existing session's `production_state.json` before moving anything.
2. Determine the source and target explicitly. For example:

```text
source: video-agent/video-generation-session/cyber_cat_trailer_20260503/
target: video-agent/video-generation-session/cyber_cat_trailer/cyber_cat_trailer_no1_20260503/
```

3. Check that the source exists and the target does not already exist or is empty. Do not overwrite a non-empty target silently.
4. Move the directory with a filesystem move/rename, then verify the source is gone and the target exists with the expected file count.
5. Batch-rewrite text artifacts under the moved session root for:
   - old session id strings, e.g. `cyber_cat_trailer_20260503` -> `cyber_cat_trailer_no1_20260503`;
   - old relative paths, e.g. `video-agent/video-generation-session/cyber_cat_trailer_20260503` -> `video-agent/video-generation-session/cyber_cat_trailer/cyber_cat_trailer_no1_20260503`;
   - old absolute paths under `/Users/.../video-agent/video-generation-session/...`.
6. Also search project-local video-agent docs/skills for concrete references to the moved session and update examples or demo references where appropriate. Keep generic legacy-layout documentation generic rather than replacing it with a concrete new-layout path.
7. Update `production_state.json` metadata such as `project_id`, `project_root`, and `updated_at` if schema-compatible, and append a `history` entry noting the migration. Do not change creative stage statuses unless actual creative artifacts were regenerated.
8. Append a migration note to `reports/production_log.md` with old path, new path, and a note that no creative stage was regenerated.
9. Validate after migration:
   - source path no longer exists;
   - target path exists;
   - all JSON files parse;
   - current key artifacts validate against schemas (`production_state`, stage reviews, shot submissions, edit manifest, etc.);
   - key media files exist and have nonzero size;
   - `ffprobe` succeeds on rough/final cut videos;
   - no stale concrete old-session references remain in project-local text files, except deliberate historical notes if the user wants to keep them.
10. Report the new canonical session root and the key delivered media/package paths.

## Continuation / sequel session checklist

When the user wants to continue the story from a previous video-generation session, create a new numbered session under the same topic directory instead of overwriting the old session.

1. Use the same `topic_name` as the previous story series. Create the next numbered session, e.g. `video-agent/video-generation-session/cyber_cat_trailer/cyber_cat_trailer_no2_20260506/`. The older flat naming style `{original_session_name}_continuation_{yyyymmdd}` should no longer be the default.
2. Read the previous session's continuity source files before generating the new brief:

```text
previous_session/production_state.json
previous_session/brief/normalized_brief.json
previous_session/script/script_v{N}.json
previous_session/script/script_v{N}.md
previous_session/storyboard/storyboard_v{N}.json
previous_session/storyboard/storyboard_v{N}.md
previous_session/assets/assets_manifest_v{N}.json
previous_session/reports/production_package.md
```

3. If the user says to continue from the last storyboard shot, extract from the previous storyboard:
   - final scene state;
   - final shot visual/action/voiceover;
   - unresolved mysteries;
   - character and prop states;
   - continuity constraints that must not be contradicted.
4. The new continuation brief should record `source_session_id`, `source_script_version`, `source_storyboard_version`, `continuation_starting_point`, and `continuity_constraints` either in `brief/normalized_brief.json` if schema-compatible or in an additional project-local continuity artifact.
5. Stop at the continuation brief human-review gate. Do not jump directly into script generation unless the user explicitly approves the continuation brief.
6. Keep the old session immutable except for optional cross-reference notes; all new story/script/storyboard/assets/shots/edit/package artifacts belong under the new continuation session root.

## Package stage checklist

When the user approves assets/edit or explicitly accepts the current rough cut as complete and asks to enter package:

1. Re-read `production_state.json`, `assets/review_v{N}.json`, and the key upstream artifacts needed for a human-readable production summary: approved brief JSON/Markdown, approved script JSON/Markdown, approved storyboard JSON/Markdown/CSV, approved assets manifest JSON/Markdown, and existing human review log.
2. Confirm `stages.assets.status == "pending_human_review"`, the assets review gate is `verdict=pass` with `blocking_issues=[]`, and `brief`, `script`, `storyboard`, and `assets` are not stale.
3. Append the assets approval to both `production_state.json.human_approvals` and `reviews/human_review_log.md` before generating the package.
4. Generate:

```text
reports/production_package.md
```

5. The package should include project info, original request, normalized brief summary, script/storyboard/assets paths and summaries, review result table, human approval/rejection history, MVP scope caveats, next-step recommendations, and a complete artifact index.
6. Preserve meaningful revision history in the package. If an earlier approved-by-review artifact was later human-rejected, summarize why and identify the approved replacement used downstream.
7. Update `production_state.json` so `stages.assets.status == "approved"`, `stages.package.status == "complete"`, `stages.package.version == "v001"`, `stages.package.artifacts == ["reports/production_package.md"]`, and `current_stage == "package_complete"`.
8. Validate `production_state.json` after the update and verify `reports/production_package.md` exists and is non-empty.
9. Read back the beginning of `reports/production_package.md`, relevant `production_state.json` state lines, and the human review log entry before responding.

## Business-user presentation and dashboard UX checklist

When the user says the video-agent workflow is too technical, file-heavy, English-heavy, hard to visualize, or asks to see scripts/storyboards/images/video clips directly in the current conversation/interface, treat this as a business-user presentation layer problem layered on top of the existing file workflow. Do not remove JSON/schema artifacts; add a concise Chinese review surface that hides technical detail by default.

Recommended class-level approach:

1. Keep `video-producer` as the stage/gate owner and keep this file workflow as the source of truth for artifacts, schemas, and approval history.
2. Add or use a presentation skill such as `video-agent-chat-presenter` for current-chat rendering:
   - Chinese-first output.
   - Show only the current decision card by default.
   - Use short cards and tables for script, storyboard, assets, shot tasks, and shot effect review.
   - Avoid exposing full prompts, provider responses, schemas, credentials, or long English machine fields unless the user asks for technical details.
3. Add or use a dashboard skill such as `video-agent-business-dashboard` for browser/Web/dashboard rendering:
   - Generate `dashboard/current_review.md` as the single business-user entrypoint.
   - Generate `dashboard/session_overview.md` for stage progress.
   - Optionally generate HTML pages such as `dashboard/current_review.html`, `dashboard/storyboard_table.html`, `dashboard/media_gallery.html`, and shot effect pages.
4. Explain interface capability honestly:
   - CLI chat can render text, summaries, tables, absolute paths, and compact review cards; it cannot reliably inline-play video or display rich images in all terminals.
   - Hermes TUI renders a Markdown subset (headings, lists, tables, code, links). Local image/video Markdown is not a true rich media preview; for example image syntax may be reduced to a textual `[image: ...]` marker.
   - Messaging gateway platforms can often deliver images/videos as media attachments. For Hermes gateway delivery, include a standalone `MEDIA:/absolute/path/to/file.mp4` or `MEDIA:/absolute/path/to/image.png` tag in the assistant response; platform adapters extract it and route common extensions such as mp4/mov/webm/png/jpg to native media send methods when the adapter supports them.
   - Hermes Dashboard/Web UI can support richer inline previews or side panels, but inspect the concrete WebUI implementation before promising media playback. In the user's `hermes-webui-master` checkout, chat Markdown already supports `MEDIA:/absolute/path/to/image.png` via `static/ui.js` + `/api/media`, rendering common image extensions as `<img class="msg-media-img">` with a lightbox. The right workspace panel also previews images via `static/workspace.js` + `/api/file/raw`. However, local video/audio files such as `.mp4`, `.mov`, `.webm`, and `.mp3` are currently treated as downloads in workspace preview and as `msg-media-link` attachments for `MEDIA:` tokens, not inline players. If business users need in-WebUI shot review playback, add a small WebUI enhancement rather than reworking the whole video-agent pipeline: add video/audio MIME entries in `api/config.py`, allow safe inline serving in `api/routes.py` where appropriate, add `VIDEO_EXTS`/`AUDIO_EXTS` preview handling in `static/workspace.js`, and render `MEDIA:/path/to/clip.mp4` as `<video controls>` in `static/ui.js`.
5. Before adding dashboard metadata to `production_state.json`, inspect `video-agent/schemas/production-state.schema.json`. If the schema has `additionalProperties: false` and no dashboard field, do not invent one during a UX/dashboard rollout; keep presentation state in `dashboard/dashboard_state.json` until the schema and validators are deliberately migrated.
6. For each human review gate, provide a single business-facing card with:
   - `当前需要你审核什么`
   - `一句话判断`
   - `你需要看什么`
   - `推荐决定`
   - `如果要修改，可以怎么说`
   - `自动审核结果摘要`
   - key absolute paths only when useful.
6. For storyboard, prefer a compact Chinese table:

```text
| 镜头 | 时长 | 画面 | 角色/资产 | 旁白/字幕 | 审核重点 |
```

7. For shot/video effect review, include:
   - local video path or media handle;
   - intended creative outcome;
   - technical verification summary such as resolution/duration/codec;
   - exactly 2-3 suggested replies, e.g. approve, revise, pause.
8. If the user pauses generation to discuss UX, record the pause in `production_state.json.human_approvals`, `reviews/human_review_log.md`, and `reports/production_log.md`; do not submit the next shot until the user explicitly resumes.
9. If a richer in-chat media experience is requested for Hermes itself, load the `hermes-agent` skill and consider whether the right implementation target is the CLI, a messaging gateway, or the Dashboard/TUI side panel. In the Dashboard, do not reimplement the main chat transcript; add structured supporting UI around the embedded TUI when possible.

Minimal viable improvement:

```text
dashboard/current_review.md
```

A good first rollout is: generate `dashboard/current_review.md` and render the same content directly in the assistant reply at every review gate, while keeping all engineering files available for traceability.

## Human review prompt format

When stopping for review, include:

```text
{stage} {version} 已生成并通过自动审核。

主要产物：
- {path_to_md}
- {path_to_json}
- {path_to_review_md}
- {path_to_review_json}

自动审核：pass，score={score}
blocking_issues=[]

请审核：
1. 回复“批准 {stage}，进入 {next_stage} 阶段。”
2. 回复“修改：...”让我返工当前阶段。
3. 回复“终止”暂停本次视频生成会话。
```

Do not proceed to the next stage in the same turn after presenting this prompt.

## Approving a stage

When the user approves a stage:

1. Re-read `production_state.json`.
2. Confirm current stage is `pending_human_review` and review gate is pass.
3. Append to `production_state.json.human_approvals`.
4. Append to `reviews/human_review_log.md`.
5. Change the approved stage status to `approved`.
6. Advance `current_stage` to the next stage.
7. Generate only the next stage requested by the user, then stop at its human-review gate.

## Revising a stage after human feedback

When the user rejects or questions a pending artifact with feedback like “修改：...” or points out a continuity/schema issue:

1. Treat the current version as not approved. Do not advance to the next stage.
2. Re-read `production_state.json`, the current stage artifact JSON/Markdown, and the relevant schema/reviewer doc before editing.
3. Append a human decision entry for the rejected version to both:

```text
production_state.json.human_approvals
reviews/human_review_log.md
```

Use `decision: "rejected"` and preserve the user's feedback verbatim in the comment.

4. Generate the next version number for the same stage, e.g. `script_v002.md/json` and `script/review_v002.md/json`.
5. Update `production_state.json.versions.{stage}`, `stages.{stage}.version`, `stages.{stage}.artifacts`, and `artifacts.{stage}` to point at the new current version. Preserve prior versions under a `previous_versions` list or in history/logs when helpful.
6. Re-run automated review for the new version. Only request human review if:

```text
verdict = pass
blocking_issues = []
```

7. Leave `current_stage` unchanged and keep the stage status `pending_human_review` for the new version.
8. If downstream artifacts already exist, mark each downstream stage `stale` with a clear `stale_reason`. If downstream stages are still `not_started`, no stale marking is required.
9. Read back the revised Markdown and review Markdown before responding so the human-facing change is verified.

## Shot request-planning implementation pitfalls

When generating `shots/shot_tasks_v{N}.*`, prefer the project-local shot generator script if it is current, but audit it before use. In the two-level session layout `video-agent/video-generation-session/{topic}/{session_id}/`, a Python script resolving `video_root` from `session_root` should use `session_root.parents[2]` (the `video-agent/` directory), not `parents[1]` (the `video-generation-session/` directory). The exact schema file is `schemas/shot-generation-tasks.schema.json`; do not look for a shorter `shot-tasks.schema.json` name. Credential placeholders must be explicit environment-variable placeholders such as `AUTH_ENV = "ARK_API_KEY"` and `Authorization: Bearer ${ARK_API_KEY}`; never use `***` in generated JSON if the schema requires `^Bearer \${[A-Z0-9_]+}$`.

If the generator script fails or is stale, it is acceptable to manually generate the same four artifacts with a small `execute_code` Python script, but still validate both `shot_tasks_v{N}.json` and `review_v{N}.json`, update `production_state.json`, append `human_review_log.md` and `production_log.md`, and stop at `stages.shots.status == "pending_human_review"` without submitting real video jobs.

## Automated review pitfalls

When implementing reviewer checks with keyword/string scans, avoid treating negated or exclusionary phrasing as evidence of a blocking issue.

Example: if storyboard text says “不是实体芯片”, “no physical chip visible”, or a `negative_prompt` says “physical chip in hand”, that should not be counted as a physical-chip handoff. Reviewers should inspect the semantic context and preferably scan only affirmative fields for positive evidence of the prohibited event.

Recommended pattern:

1. Validate schemas and references first.
2. For continuity checks, separate affirmative content fields (`scene`, `visual`, `character_action`, `dialogue_or_voiceover`, asset descriptions) from exclusion fields (`negative_prompt`) and explicitly negated phrases.
3. Flag blocking issues only for affirmative violations, e.g. “把实体芯片交给”, “芯片交到主角手里”, “主角拿走芯片”.
4. If a reviewer initially over-flags a negated phrase, correct the review JSON/Markdown, update `production_state.json.stages.{stage}.status` back to `pending_human_review` only if the final review gate is `verdict=pass` and `blocking_issues=[]`, and append a history entry explaining the correction.
5. Re-run schema/state validation after the correction and read back the review Markdown before presenting it to the user.

## Final validation for a demo run

A demo is considered run through when:

1. Files are present and non-empty.
2. All JSON files parse.
3. Stage JSON files validate against their schemas.
4. Every review JSON on the current approved path has `verdict=pass` and `blocking_issues=[]`. Historical rejected versions may remain for auditability; validate their schemas but do not treat them as current-path gates.
5. Human approvals/rejections are recorded in both `production_state.json.human_approvals` and `reviews/human_review_log.md`.
6. `production_state.json` shows coherent stage progression: approved stages are `approved`, package is `complete`, and `current_stage == "package_complete"` after package generation.
7. No stale artifact is used downstream.
8. `reports/production_package.md` exists and is non-empty once the package stage is reached.

## Implementation tips

- Use `read_file` for project-local docs and schemas.
- Use `terminal date +%Y%m%d` for the current date.
- Prefer a small Python script via `execute_code` for multi-file JSON writing and schema validation.
- Do not use shell heredocs or `cat` to create files; use `write_file`, `patch`, or `execute_code` with pathlib.
- After writing artifacts, read back key Markdown and JSON files to verify the human-facing content and state.
- Keep all paths explicit in the final response.
- For provider CLI downloads (Dreamina, etc.), resolve download directories to absolute paths with `Path(session_root).resolve()` before calling the CLI. Store both session-relative artifact paths for portability and absolute local media paths for immediate user inspection when the file is generated.
- If the parent workspace path is renamed, audit session JSON/Markdown for stale absolute paths and update project-local docs/scripts; a compatibility symlink from the old path to the new path can keep historical media references working during migration.

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Submit one video shot through Dreamina CLI.

The script submits at most one shot per invocation. It blocks if any previous
shot is still pending generation/effect review.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import datetime as dt
import json
import re
import shutil
import subprocess
from pathlib import Path

SUPPORTED_MODEL_VERSIONS = {"seedance2.0", "seedance2.0fast", "seedance2.0_vip", "seedance2.0fast_vip"}
SUPPORTED_GENERATOR_COMMANDS = {
    "frames2video",
    "image2image",
    "image2video",
    "image_upscale",
    "multiframe2video",
    "multimodal2video",
    "text2image",
    "text2video",
}
DEFAULT_MODEL_VERSION = "seedance2.0fast_vip"



def utc_now() -> str:
    return dt.datetime.utcnow().replace(microsecond=0).isoformat() + "Z"


def load_json(path: Path):
    return json.loads(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))


def write_json(path: Path, data) -> None:
    path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    path.write_text(json.dumps(data, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2) + "\n", encoding="utf-8")


def choose_generator_command(task: dict, requested: str) -> str:
    """Choose the Dreamina generator command for a shot task.

    Current shot task packages are text-first, so the auto fallback is
    text2video. Future packages may set task["dreamina"]["generator_command"]
    or provide reference images/video/audio, in which case this selector can
    use the richer Dreamina commands without changing the producer flow.
    """
    if requested != "auto":
        return requested
    configured = (task.get("dreamina") or {}).get("generator_command")
    if configured in SUPPORTED_GENERATOR_COMMANDS:
        return configured
    references = task.get("reference_media") or task.get("references") or []
    if isinstance(references, list):
        image_refs = [r for r in references if isinstance(r, dict) and r.get("type") in {"image", "reference_image"}]
        video_refs = [r for r in references if isinstance(r, dict) and r.get("type") in {"video", "reference_video"}]
        audio_refs = [r for r in references if isinstance(r, dict) and r.get("type") in {"audio", "reference_audio"}]
        if len(image_refs) >= 2 and not video_refs and not audio_refs:
            return "multiframe2video"
        if len(image_refs) == 1 and not video_refs and not audio_refs:
            return "image2video"
        if image_refs or video_refs or audio_refs:
            return "multimodal2video"
    return "text2video"


def extract_submit_id(text: str):
    patterns = [r'"submit_id"\s*:\s*"([^"]+)"', r'"submitId"\s*:\s*"([^"]+)"', r'submit[_ ]id[=:： ]+([A-Za-z0-9_-]+)']
    for pat in patterns:
        m = re.search(pat, text, re.I)
        if m:
            return m.group(1)
    return None


def main() -> int:
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    parser.add_argument("--session-root", required=True)
    parser.add_argument("--version", default="v001")
    parser.add_argument("--poll", type=int, default=30)
    parser.add_argument("--video-resolution", default="720p")
    parser.add_argument("--model-version", default=DEFAULT_MODEL_VERSION, choices=sorted(SUPPORTED_MODEL_VERSIONS))
    parser.add_argument("--generator-command", default="auto", choices=["auto"] + sorted(SUPPORTED_GENERATOR_COMMANDS))
    parser.add_argument("--model-confirmed", action="store_true", help="Required for real submissions after the user confirms the model choice.")
    parser.add_argument("--dry-run", action="store_true")
    args = parser.parse_args()

    session = Path(args.session_root).resolve()
    shots_dir = session / "shots"
    shot_tasks = load_json(shots_dir / f"shot_tasks_{args.version}.json")
    review = load_json(shots_dir / f"review_{args.version}.json")
    if review.get("verdict") != "pass" or review.get("blocking_issues") != []:
        raise SystemExit("shot task review gate is not pass")

    now = utc_now()
    submission_path = shots_dir / f"dreamina_submission_{args.version}.json"
    if submission_path.exists():
        submission = load_json(submission_path)
    else:
        submission = {
            "schema_version": "0.1.0",
            "session_id": shot_tasks["session_id"],
            "version": args.version,
            "source_shot_tasks_version": shot_tasks["version"],
            "submission_mode": "dreamina_cli_sequential_one_shot_at_a_time",
            "current_shot_id": None,
            "submissions": [],
            "next_action": "submit first shot only",
            "created_at": now,
            "updated_at": now,
        }

    blocking = {"submitted", "querying", "generation_pending", "generation_succeeded", "pending_human_effect_review"}
    for existing in submission["submissions"]:
        if existing.get("status") in blocking:
            print(json.dumps({"blocked": True, "reason": "previous shot needs completion/human review", "shot_id": existing.get("shot_id")}, ensure_ascii=False))
            return 2

    completed = {s["shot_id"] for s in submission["submissions"] if s.get("status") == "human_approved"}
    task = next((t for t in shot_tasks["tasks"] if t["shot_id"] not in completed), None)
    if task is None:
        print(json.dumps({"done": True, "message": "all shots approved"}, ensure_ascii=False))
        return 0

    generator_command = choose_generator_command(task, args.generator_command)
    if not args.dry_run and not args.model_confirmed:
        print(json.dumps({
            "blocked": True,
            "reason": "model confirmation required before real Dreamina submission",
            "shot_id": task["shot_id"],
            "recommended_model_version": args.model_version,
            "generator_command": generator_command,
            "next_action": "ask the user to confirm model_version before rerunning with --model-confirmed",
        }, ensure_ascii=False))
        return 3

    response_dir = shots_dir / "dreamina_responses"
    response_dir.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
    response_rel = f"shots/dreamina_responses/{task['shot_id']}_submit_response_{args.version}.json"
    response_path = session / response_rel

    dreamina_path = shutil.which("dreamina")
    cmd = [
        "dreamina", generator_command,
        f"--prompt={task['prompt_text']}",
        f"--duration={task['duration']}",
        f"--ratio={task['request']['body'].get('ratio', '9:16')}",
        f"--video_resolution={args.video_resolution}",
        f"--model_version={args.model_version}",
        f"--poll={args.poll}",
    ]

    if args.dry_run:
        result = {
            "dry_run": True,
            "command": cmd,
            "stdout": "",
            "stderr": "",
            "returncode": 0,
            "created_at": now,
        }
        status = "dry_run"
    elif not dreamina_path:
        result = {
            "error": "dreamina command not found",
            "command": cmd,
            "stdout": "",
            "stderr": "dreamina command not found; install with curl -fsSL https://jimeng.jianying.com/cli | bash",
            "returncode": 127,
            "created_at": now,
        }
        status = "submission_failed"
    else:
        proc = subprocess.run(cmd, text=True, capture_output=True, timeout=max(args.poll + 60, 120))
        result = {
            "command": cmd,
            "stdout": proc.stdout,
            "stderr": proc.stderr,
            "returncode": proc.returncode,
            "created_at": now,
        }
        status = "submitted" if proc.returncode == 0 else "submission_failed"

    combined = (result.get("stdout") or "") + "\n" + (result.get("stderr") or "")
    submit_id = extract_submit_id(combined)
    video_urls = re.findall(r'https?://[^\s"\']+', combined)
    video_url = video_urls[0] if video_urls else None
    if status == "submitted" and ("querying" in combined.lower() or submit_id):
        status = "querying"
    if status == "submitted" and video_url:
        status = "pending_human_effect_review"

    write_json(response_path, result)
    record = {
        "shot_id": task["shot_id"],
        "task_id": task["task_id"],
        "order": task["order"],
        "status": status,
        "submitted_at": now,
        "cli": "dreamina",
        "generator_command": generator_command,
        "model_version": args.model_version,
        "model_confirmed": bool(args.model_confirmed),
        "command_preview": "dreamina {} --prompt=<redacted_prompt> --duration={} --ratio={} --video_resolution={} --model_version={} --poll={}".format(generator_command, task["duration"], task["request"]["body"].get("ratio", "9:16"), args.video_resolution, args.model_version, args.poll),
        "submit_id": submit_id,
        "response_json_path": response_rel,
        "video_url": video_url,
        "local_video_path": None,
        "error_summary": None if status in {"submitted", "querying", "pending_human_effect_review", "dry_run"} else result.get("stderr") or result.get("error"),
    }
    submission["submissions"].append(record)
    submission["current_shot_id"] = task["shot_id"]
    submission["updated_at"] = now
    submission["next_action"] = "query/download and human-review current shot before next shot" if status in {"submitted", "querying", "pending_human_effect_review"} else "fix current shot submission; do not continue next shot"
    write_json(submission_path, submission)

    md = [
        f"# Dreamina Sequential Shot Submission {args.version}",
        "",
        f"- Session ID: {submission['session_id']}",
        "- Mode: dreamina_cli_sequential_one_shot_at_a_time",
        "- Policy: submit one shot only, then stop for result/effect review.",
        f"- Current shot: {task['shot_id']}",
        f"- Current task: {task['task_id']}",
        f"- Status: {status}",
        f"- Generator command: {generator_command}",
        f"- Model version: {args.model_version}",
        f"- Model confirmed: {bool(args.model_confirmed)}",
        f"- Submit ID: {submit_id or 'not_available_yet'}",
        f"- Video URL: {video_url or 'not_available_yet'}",
        f"- Response JSON: {response_rel}",
        "",
        "## Gate",
        "",
        "Do not submit the next shot until the current shot is generated and approved by the user.",
    ]
    (shots_dir / f"dreamina_submission_{args.version}.md").write_text("\n".join(md) + "\n", encoding="utf-8")
    print(json.dumps(record, ensure_ascii=False))
    return 0 if status in {"submitted", "querying", "pending_human_effect_review", "dry_run"} else 1


if __name__ == "__main__":
    raise SystemExit(main())

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Submit the next Doubao Seedance shot sequentially.

This script submits at most one shot per invocation. It never writes ARK_API_KEY
into artifacts. It updates shots/submission_v001.json and writes the provider
response under shots/provider_responses/.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import datetime as _dt
import json
import os
from pathlib import Path
import urllib.error
import urllib.request


def utc_now() -> str:
    return _dt.datetime.utcnow().replace(microsecond=0).isoformat() + "Z"


def load_json(path: Path):
    return json.loads(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))


def write_json(path: Path, data) -> None:
    path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    path.write_text(json.dumps(data, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2) + "\n", encoding="utf-8")


def extract_handles(response_obj):
    provider_task_id = None
    provider_status = None
    video_url = None

    def walk(obj):
        if isinstance(obj, dict):
            yield obj
            for v in obj.values():
                yield from walk(v)
        elif isinstance(obj, list):
            for v in obj:
                yield from walk(v)

    if isinstance(response_obj, dict):
        for d in walk(response_obj):
            for key in ("id", "task_id", "taskId", "generation_id"):
                if provider_task_id is None and isinstance(d.get(key), str):
                    provider_task_id = d[key]
            for key in ("status", "state"):
                if provider_status is None and isinstance(d.get(key), str):
                    provider_status = d[key]
            for key in ("url", "video_url", "videoUrl"):
                val = d.get(key)
                if video_url is None and isinstance(val, str) and val.startswith(("http://", "https://")):
                    video_url = val
    return provider_task_id, provider_status, video_url


def main() -> int:
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    parser.add_argument("--session-root", required=True)
    parser.add_argument("--version", default="v001")
    parser.add_argument("--dry-run", action="store_true")
    args = parser.parse_args()

    session = Path(args.session_root).resolve()
    shots_dir = session / "shots"
    shot_tasks = load_json(shots_dir / f"shot_tasks_{args.version}.json")
    review = load_json(shots_dir / f"review_{args.version}.json")
    if review.get("verdict") != "pass" or review.get("blocking_issues") != []:
        raise SystemExit("shot task review gate is not pass")

    now = utc_now()
    submission_path = shots_dir / f"submission_{args.version}.json"
    if submission_path.exists():
        submission = load_json(submission_path)
    else:
        submission = {
            "schema_version": "0.1.0",
            "session_id": shot_tasks["session_id"],
            "version": args.version,
            "source_shot_tasks_version": shot_tasks["version"],
            "submission_mode": "sequential_one_shot_at_a_time",
            "current_shot_id": None,
            "submissions": [],
            "next_action": "submit first shot only",
            "created_at": now,
            "updated_at": now,
        }

    blocking_statuses = {"submitted", "generation_pending", "generation_succeeded", "pending_human_effect_review"}
    for existing in submission["submissions"]:
        if existing.get("status") in blocking_statuses:
            print(json.dumps({"blocked": True, "reason": "previous shot needs completion/human review", "shot_id": existing.get("shot_id")}, ensure_ascii=False))
            return 2

    completed = {s["shot_id"] for s in submission["submissions"] if s.get("status") == "human_approved"}
    task = next((t for t in shot_tasks["tasks"] if t["shot_id"] not in completed), None)
    if task is None:
        print(json.dumps({"done": True, "message": "all shots approved"}, ensure_ascii=False))
        return 0

    api_key = os.environ.get("ARK_API_KEY")
    response_dir = shots_dir / "provider_responses"
    response_dir.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
    response_rel = f"shots/provider_responses/{task['shot_id']}_submit_response_{args.version}.json"
    response_path = session / response_rel

    status_code = None
    err_summary = None
    response_obj = None
    status_text = "submission_failed"

    if args.dry_run:
        response_obj = {"dry_run": True, "submitted": False, "created_at": now}
        err_summary = "dry run; no external API call submitted"
    elif not api_key:
        response_obj = {"error": "ARK_API_KEY is not set", "submitted": False, "created_at": now}
        err_summary = "ARK_API_KEY is not set in environment; no external API call submitted."
    else:
        req = urllib.request.Request(
            task["request"]["url"],
            data=json.dumps(task["request"]["body"], ensure_ascii=False).encode("utf-8"),
            method="POST",
            headers={"Content-Type": "application/json", "Authorization": f"Bearer {api_key}"},
        )
        try:
            with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=120) as resp:
                status_code = resp.status
                raw = resp.read().decode("utf-8", errors="replace")
            try:
                response_obj = json.loads(raw)
            except Exception:
                response_obj = {"raw_response": raw}
            status_text = "submitted"
        except urllib.error.HTTPError as e:
            status_code = e.code
            raw = e.read().decode("utf-8", errors="replace")
            try:
                response_obj = json.loads(raw)
            except Exception:
                response_obj = {"raw_response": raw}
            err_summary = f"HTTP {e.code}"
        except Exception as e:
            response_obj = {"error": type(e).__name__, "message": str(e)}
            err_summary = f"{type(e).__name__}: {e}"

    provider_task_id, provider_status, video_url = extract_handles(response_obj)
    write_json(response_path, {"submitted_at": now, "shot_id": task["shot_id"], "task_id": task["task_id"], "http_status": status_code, "response": response_obj})

    record = {
        "shot_id": task["shot_id"],
        "task_id": task["task_id"],
        "order": task["order"],
        "status": status_text,
        "submitted_at": now,
        "request_url": task["request"]["url"],
        "response_status_code": status_code,
        "provider_task_id": provider_task_id,
        "provider_status": provider_status,
        "response_json_path": response_rel,
        "video_url": video_url,
        "local_video_path": None,
        "error_summary": err_summary,
    }
    submission["submissions"].append(record)
    submission["current_shot_id"] = task["shot_id"]
    submission["updated_at"] = now
    submission["next_action"] = "poll/download and human-review current shot before next shot" if status_text == "submitted" else "fix current shot submission; do not continue next shot"
    write_json(submission_path, submission)

    md = [
        f"# Sequential Shot Submission {args.version}",
        "",
        f"- Session ID: {submission['session_id']}",
        "- Mode: sequential_one_shot_at_a_time",
        "- Policy: submit one shot only, then stop for result/effect review.",
        f"- Current shot: {task['shot_id']}",
        f"- Current task: {task['task_id']}",
        f"- Status: {status_text}",
        f"- HTTP status: {status_code}",
        f"- Provider task id: {provider_task_id or 'not_available_yet'}",
        f"- Provider status: {provider_status or 'not_available_yet'}",
        f"- Video URL: {video_url or 'not_available_yet'}",
        f"- Response JSON: {response_rel}",
        "",
        "## Gate",
        "",
        "Do not submit the next shot until the current shot is generated and approved by the user.",
    ]
    (shots_dir / f"submission_{args.version}.md").write_text("\n".join(md) + "\n", encoding="utf-8")
    print(json.dumps(record, ensure_ascii=False))
    return 0 if status_text == "submitted" else 1


if __name__ == "__main__":
    raise SystemExit(main())
